Surah: 76. Follow the brief below (surah_map.md) exactly. The evidence is text.md (the surah), dictionary.md (every root of the surah's words, each branch with the classical dictionaries' own phrases), channels.md and hft.md (both are earlier readers' proposals: ignore their judgements (grades, strength or confidence labels, reading types, statements of what a reading may or may not do) and make your own) and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Return only the map as your final message.

===== _commentary/v16/prompts/r2/surah_map.md =====
Read the surah as one text and write its map of image chains: the lexical images that run through several of
its ayat and join them into one scene, process or movement. A later writer will read one ayah at a time, with
only that ayah's own dictionary. The map lets that writer hear what the ayah's words carry in the surah as a
whole, including senses whose evidence sits under the words of other ayat.

Your evidence is the surah text, the dictionary of every root in the surah (each branch with the classical
dictionaries' own phrases), earlier readers' channel review (channels.md) and activation hypotheses (hft.md),
and your own knowledge of Arabic and the Quran. Where a member or passage comes from memory rather than from
the dictionary or the text, say so. Do not use tools, delegate, browse or inspect files.

The channel review and the HFT records are proposals by earlier readers. Ignore their judgements: grades,
strength or confidence labels, reading types, words such as "surprising", "exploratory" or "latent", and every
statement of what a reading may or may not do. Make your own judgement from the surah's words, the dictionary
phrases and the Quran. Do not rediscover what they already assembled: start from their chains, test each one
against the dictionary phrases and the text, and join, extend, split or correct them. Where their wording
abstracts a member, go back to the dictionary phrase and name what it actually says.

A chain belongs on the map when its members are senses the dictionary attests for words that stand in the
surah, and together they make one image or process that the surah's wording or sequence lets a listener hear.
A member need not be the sense that translates its word in its own ayah; a chain is heard across the surah,
not in one word. Say plainly when a member is a rare sense or is attested only inside a fixed expression, and
keep it. When the dictionary itself joins two of the surah's words in one phrase, quote that phrase: it is
the strongest evidence a chain can have. Keep a scene at the level of its objects, their parts and their
operation. When proposals share members, do not fold one into another's more abstract function unless nothing
concrete is lost. Carry every chain that meets this test, however unusual; leave out proposals that do not.

Write the map in English, with Arabic quoted exactly (surah wording from the text, dictionary phrases from the
dictionary). It is working material for the writer, not commentary prose.

1. `## Chains`. For each chain, a `###` heading naming the image. One paragraph on what the image is and how it
   moves through the surah. Then its members, one line each: the ayah ref, the word as it stands in the text,
   the root and branch id, the dictionary's own phrase quoted exactly, and what this member contributes to the
   image. Add Quran passages outside the surah, with exact refs, where they stage or confirm the chain.
2. `## Ayat`. For each ayah in order: which chains it opens, advances or completes, and what its words add to
   each.
3. `## Not carried`. Each channel subchannel and each HFT record you did not carry into a chain, one line each:
   its name and why.

No ranking and no labels of strength or confidence. No list of what a writer must include. There is no length
target and no required number of chains or members.


===== _commentary/v16/work/s076/surah.r2/text.md =====
# Surah 76

- 76:1 هَلْ أَتَىٰ عَلَى ٱلْإِنسَٰنِ حِينٌۭ مِّنَ ٱلدَّهْرِ لَمْ يَكُن شَيْـًۭٔا مَّذْكُورًا
- 76:2 إِنَّا خَلَقْنَا ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ مِن نُّطْفَةٍ أَمْشَاجٍۢ نَّبْتَلِيهِ فَجَعَلْنَٰهُ سَمِيعًۢا بَصِيرًا
- 76:3 إِنَّا هَدَيْنَٰهُ ٱلسَّبِيلَ إِمَّا شَاكِرًۭا وَإِمَّا كَفُورًا
- 76:4 إِنَّآ أَعْتَدْنَا لِلْكَٰفِرِينَ سَلَٰسِلَا۟ وَأَغْلَٰلًۭا وَسَعِيرًا
- 76:5 إِنَّ ٱلْأَبْرَارَ يَشْرَبُونَ مِن كَأْسٍۢ كَانَ مِزَاجُهَا كَافُورًا
- 76:6 عَيْنًۭا يَشْرَبُ بِهَا عِبَادُ ٱللَّهِ يُفَجِّرُونَهَا تَفْجِيرًۭا
- 76:7 يُوفُونَ بِٱلنَّذْرِ وَيَخَافُونَ يَوْمًۭا كَانَ شَرُّهُۥ مُسْتَطِيرًۭا
- 76:8 وَيُطْعِمُونَ ٱلطَّعَامَ عَلَىٰ حُبِّهِۦ مِسْكِينًۭا وَيَتِيمًۭا وَأَسِيرًا
- 76:9 إِنَّمَا نُطْعِمُكُمْ لِوَجْهِ ٱللَّهِ لَا نُرِيدُ مِنكُمْ جَزَآءًۭ وَلَا شُكُورًا
- 76:10 إِنَّا نَخَافُ مِن رَّبِّنَا يَوْمًا عَبُوسًۭا قَمْطَرِيرًۭا
- 76:11 فَوَقَىٰهُمُ ٱللَّهُ شَرَّ ذَٰلِكَ ٱلْيَوْمِ وَلَقَّىٰهُمْ نَضْرَةًۭ وَسُرُورًۭا
- 76:12 وَجَزَىٰهُم بِمَا صَبَرُوا۟ جَنَّةًۭ وَحَرِيرًۭا
- 76:13 مُّتَّكِـِٔينَ فِيهَا عَلَى ٱلْأَرَآئِكِ ۖ لَا يَرَوْنَ فِيهَا شَمْسًۭا وَلَا زَمْهَرِيرًۭا
- 76:14 وَدَانِيَةً عَلَيْهِمْ ظِلَٰلُهَا وَذُلِّلَتْ قُطُوفُهَا تَذْلِيلًۭا
- 76:15 وَيُطَافُ عَلَيْهِم بِـَٔانِيَةٍۢ مِّن فِضَّةٍۢ وَأَكْوَابٍۢ كَانَتْ قَوَارِيرَا۠
- 76:16 قَوَارِيرَا۟ مِن فِضَّةٍۢ قَدَّرُوهَا تَقْدِيرًۭا
- 76:17 وَيُسْقَوْنَ فِيهَا كَأْسًۭا كَانَ مِزَاجُهَا زَنجَبِيلًا
- 76:18 عَيْنًۭا فِيهَا تُسَمَّىٰ سَلْسَبِيلًۭا
- 76:19 ۞ وَيَطُوفُ عَلَيْهِمْ وِلْدَٰنٌۭ مُّخَلَّدُونَ إِذَا رَأَيْتَهُمْ حَسِبْتَهُمْ لُؤْلُؤًۭا مَّنثُورًۭا
- 76:20 وَإِذَا رَأَيْتَ ثَمَّ رَأَيْتَ نَعِيمًۭا وَمُلْكًۭا كَبِيرًا
- 76:21 عَٰلِيَهُمْ ثِيَابُ سُندُسٍ خُضْرٌۭ وَإِسْتَبْرَقٌۭ ۖ وَحُلُّوٓا۟ أَسَاوِرَ مِن فِضَّةٍۢ وَسَقَىٰهُمْ رَبُّهُمْ شَرَابًۭا طَهُورًا
- 76:22 إِنَّ هَٰذَا كَانَ لَكُمْ جَزَآءًۭ وَكَانَ سَعْيُكُم مَّشْكُورًا
- 76:23 إِنَّا نَحْنُ نَزَّلْنَا عَلَيْكَ ٱلْقُرْءَانَ تَنزِيلًۭا
- 76:24 فَٱصْبِرْ لِحُكْمِ رَبِّكَ وَلَا تُطِعْ مِنْهُمْ ءَاثِمًا أَوْ كَفُورًۭا
- 76:25 وَٱذْكُرِ ٱسْمَ رَبِّكَ بُكْرَةًۭ وَأَصِيلًۭا
- 76:26 وَمِنَ ٱلَّيْلِ فَٱسْجُدْ لَهُۥ وَسَبِّحْهُ لَيْلًۭا طَوِيلًا
- 76:27 إِنَّ هَٰٓؤُلَآءِ يُحِبُّونَ ٱلْعَاجِلَةَ وَيَذَرُونَ وَرَآءَهُمْ يَوْمًۭا ثَقِيلًۭا
- 76:28 نَّحْنُ خَلَقْنَٰهُمْ وَشَدَدْنَآ أَسْرَهُمْ ۖ وَإِذَا شِئْنَا بَدَّلْنَآ أَمْثَٰلَهُمْ تَبْدِيلًا
- 76:29 إِنَّ هَٰذِهِۦ تَذْكِرَةٌۭ ۖ فَمَن شَآءَ ٱتَّخَذَ إِلَىٰ رَبِّهِۦ سَبِيلًۭا
- 76:30 وَمَا تَشَآءُونَ إِلَّآ أَن يَشَآءَ ٱللَّهُ ۚ إِنَّ ٱللَّهَ كَانَ عَلِيمًا حَكِيمًۭا
- 76:31 يُدْخِلُ مَن يَشَآءُ فِى رَحْمَتِهِۦ ۚ وَٱلظَّٰلِمِينَ أَعَدَّ لَهُمْ عَذَابًا أَلِيمًۢا


===== _commentary/v16/work/s076/surah.r2/dictionary.md =====
# Dictionary: every branch of every focus root

Every root of the surah's words, each once, headed by the ayah and word where it first occurs; it also serves the later words listed with it.
One entry per branch: a Turkish label and Turkish glosses of its attested senses, then the classical dictionaries' own phrases with their source tags (ayn, sihah, tahdhib, maqayis, mufradat, jamhara). [kalıp] marks a sense the dictionaries attest only inside a fixed expression.
Identity roots come from the quran-data gateway. Word-scoped alternatives are cited analyses of this
exact word. **Echo roots** are observed but withheld mappings: sound-family candidates, not identity.

## ء ت ي (root_000009): 76:1 أَتَىٰ

- **B001** gelmek, ulaşmak — gelmek veya ulaşmak · ona gitmek veya yanına varmak · geciktiğini düşünüp gelmesini istemek
  أتى يأتي أتيا (jamhara)؛ الإتيان المجئ (sihah)؛ أتاني فلان إتيانا وأتيا وأتية وأتوة (tahdhib;maqayis)؛ الإتيان مجيء بسهولة (mufradat)
- **B002** vermek; getirip sunmak — vermek; bir şeyi getirip sunmak
  آتى يؤتي إيتاء في معنى أعطى (jamhara)؛ آتاه إيتاء أي أعطاه وآتاه أيضا أي أتى به (sihah)؛ الإتياء الإعطاء (tahdhib)؛ الإيتاء الإعطاء (mufradat;maqayis)
- **B003** uygun yoldan ele almak ve elverişli hale gelmek — işin uygun yönü ve tutulacak yolu · uyma ve razı olma · bir şeyin ona elverişli hale gelmesi · ihtiyacını uygun yoldan ve incelikle yürütmek
  أتيت الأمر من مأتاته (sihah;maqayis)؛ آتيته على ذلك الأمر مواتاة إذا وافقته وطاوعته (sihah)؛ آتيت فلانا على أمره مؤاتاة وهو حسن المطاوعة (maqayis)؛ تأتى له الشيء أي تهيأ (sihah)؛ تأتى فلان لحاجته إذا ترفق لها (tahdhib)
- **B004** su kanalı açmak ve akışı yönlendirmek — su kanalı; suyu tutan odun ve yaprak birikintisi · bu suya yol açıp akışını yönlendirmek
  أت لمائك أي سهل له سبيلا وذلك السبيل الأتي (jamhara)؛ الأتي الجدول يؤتيه الرجل إلى أرضه (sihah)؛ كل جدول ماء أتي (tahdhib)؛ أت لهذا الماء أي سهل جريه (maqayis)؛ الأتي ما وقع في النهر من خشب أو ورق مما يحبس الماء (maqayis)
- **B005** başka bölgeden gelen sel [kalıp] — yağmur alan başka bir bölgeden gelen sel
  الأتي السيل بعينه يأتيك من بلد مطر من غير بلدك (jamhara)؛ سيل أتي وأتاوي إذا جاءك ولم يصبك مطره (sihah)؛ المسيل الذي يأتي من بلد قد مطر فيه إلى بلد لم يمطر فيه أتي (tahdhib)؛ السيل المار على وجهه أتي وأتاوي (mufradat)؛ الأتي أيضا السيل الذي يأتي من بلد غير بلدك (maqayis)
- **B006** topluluğa yabancı kimse [kalıp] — içinde bulunduğu topluluğa mensup olmayan yabancı adam
  رجل أتي وأتاوي وهو الغريب (jamhara)؛ الاتي أيضا والاتاوى الغريب (sihah)؛ إنما هو أتي فينا (tahdhib)؛ به شبه الغريب فقيل أتاوي (mufradat)؛ رجل أتي أي غريب في قوم ليس منهم وأتاوي كذلك (maqayis)
- **B007** gelişip bol ürün vermek — ekin ve hurmanın gelişmesi, ürünü ve bol verimi · çalkalanan tulumun yağının ortaya çıkması
  أتاء هذا النخل أي ثمره وكذلك الزرع (jamhara)؛ الاتاء البركة والنماء وحمل النخل (sihah)؛ جاء أتوه (sihah;mufradat)؛ إتاء النخلة ريعها وزكاؤها وكثرة ثمارها (tahdhib)؛ الإتاء نماء الزرع والنخل وأتى الماء إتاء أي كثر (maqayis)
- **B008** ödenen vergi; rüşvet — vergi veya baş vergisi; rüşvet · ona rüşvet vermek
  الإتاوة الخراج أو الجزية يؤديه القوم إلى الملك (jamhara)؛ الاتاوة الخراج والجمع الاتاوي (sihah)؛ الإتاوة الخراج وجمعها الأتاوى والإتاوات (tahdhib)؛ أتوته أتوة إذا رشوته إتاوة وهي الرشوة (tahdhib)
- **B009** devenin ön ayaklarını geri getirişi [kalıp] — devenin yürürken ön ayaklarını geri getirişi
  ما أحسن أتو قوائم الناقة وأتيها في السير (jamhara)؛ ما أحسن أتو يدي هذه الناقة وأتي أيضا أي رجع يديها في السير (sihah)؛ ما أحسن أتو يديها وأتي يديها يعني رجع يديها (tahdhib)
- **B010** işlek ana yol, son sınır ve karşı hizası — yarışın son sınırı; işlek ana yol veya yol kavşağı · yarışın son sınırı veya yolun ana kesimi · bir evin karşısında veya aynı hizasında
  الميتاء والميداء آخر الغاية حيث ينتهي إليه جري الخيل (sihah)؛ الميتاء الطريق العامر ومجتمع الطريق (sihah)؛ داري بميتاء دار فلان وميداء دار فلان أي تلقاء داره ومحاذية لها (sihah)؛ طريق ميتاء مسلوك وميتاء الطريق وميداؤه محجته (tahdhib)
- **B011** felakete uğramak, kaybetmek veya düşmanca ele geçirilmek [kalıp] — ölüm, ağır hastalık, bela veya kırığa uğramak · malı yok olmak · uğruna öldürülmek, götürülmek veya yenilmek · düşman yaklaşmış olmak
  أتى على فلان أتو أي موت أو بلاء أصابه (tahdhib)؛ الأتو المرض الشديد أو كسر يد أو رجل أو موت (tahdhib)؛ أتي على يد فلان إذا هلك له مال (tahdhib)؛ يؤتى دونه أي يذهب به ويغلب عليه (tahdhib)؛ أتي فلان إذا أطل عليه العدو (tahdhib)؛ الإتيان يقال في الخير وفي الشر (mufradat)
- **B012** dişi devenin çiftleşmek istemesi [kalıp] — dişi devenin çiftleşmek için erkek deve istemesi
  استأتت الناقة استئتاء مهموز أي ضبعت وأرادت الفحل (sihah)
- **B013** etkili ve işini yürüten adam [kalıp] — etkili ve işini yürütebilen adam
  رجل أتي إذا كان نافذا (maqayis)

## ء ن س (root_000059): 76:1 ٱلْإِنسَٰنِ, 76:2 ٱلْإِنسَٰنَ

- **B001** insan türü ve bu türden bir kişi — insanlar; insan topluluğu · insan; insan türü · insan topluluğunun bir üyesi; insana veya insanlara ait · insanlar; insan toplulukları · insanlar; halk · evde hiç kimse yok · belirli bir ağızda insan ve onun çoğulu
  الإنس خلاف الجن وسموا لظهورهم (maqayis;mufradat)؛ الإنس البشر والواحد إنسي والجمع أناسي (sihah)؛ الإنس جماعة الناس والأناسي جماع (tahdhib)
- **B002** görerek fark etme; bağlama göre işitme, sezme ve bakıp araştırma — bir şeyi görmek ve fark etmek · sesi işitmek · onda olgunluk belirtisi görmek ve bunu anlamak · ürken yabani hayvanın birini sezip çevreye bakınması · çevreye bakıp birinin olup olmadığını araştırmak
  آنست الشيء إذا رأيته وآنسته إذا سمعته (maqayis)؛ آنسته أبصرته وآنست الصوت سمعته وآنست منه رشدا علمته (sihah)؛ آنس من جانب يعني أبصر نارا والاستئناس النظر وأحس بما رابه (tahdhib)؛ فإن آنستم منهم رشدا أي أبصرتم وآنست نارا (mufradat)
- **B003** yabancılık duymadan yakınlık ve rahatlık hissetme — yakınlık ve rahatlık; yabancılık duymama · birine alışıp onun yanında sevinmek · biriyle yakınlık kurmak ve onsuz kendini yalnız hissetmek · yakın arkadaş; rahatlık veren kişi veya şey · yakınlıktan ve söyleşiden hoşlanan genç kadın · insana alışık, saldırgan olmayan köpek · gece yolcusuna veya konaklayana güven veren ateş · sahibine güven veren bütün silahlar; zırh, miğfer, koruyucu örtü ve kalkan gibi savunma donanımları
  الأنس أنس الإنسان بالشيء إذا لم يستوحش منه (maqayis)؛ الإيناس خلاف الإيحاش والإنس خلاف الوحشة والأنيس المؤانس وكل ما يؤنس به (sihah)؛ أنست بفلان أي فرحت به والأنس والاستئناس هو التأنس وكلب أنوس نقيض العقور (tahdhib)؛ الأنس خلاف النفور ولكل ما يؤنس به (mufradat)
- **B004** insana dönük yan — bir şeyin insana bakan veya en yakın olan yanı · yayın okçuya bakan yüzü · hayvanın biniciye yakın olan yanı
  الإنسي الأيسر من كل شيء وقيل الأيمن وما أقبل منهما على الإنسان فهو إنسي وإنسي القوس ما أقبل عليك منها (sihah)؛ الإنسي من الدواب الجانب الأيسر الذي منه يركب ويحتلب ومن الإنسان الجانب الذي يلي الرجل الأخرى (tahdhib)؛ إنسي الدابة للجانب الذي يلي الراكب وإنسي القوس للجانب الذي يقبل على الرامي (mufradat)
- **B005** göz bebeğinde görülen küçük yansıma — göz bebeğinde görülen küçük görüntü veya yansıma · göz bebeklerinde görülen küçük görüntüler · parmak ucu; eldeki parmak ucunu anlatan kullanım

## غ ل ل (root_001102): 76:4 وَأَغْلَٰلًا

- **B001** bir şeyin içine girme veya sokup sabitleme — bir şeyi başka bir şeyin içine saplarcasına yerleştirmek · bir şeyin içine girip ortasına ulaşmak · içine sokulunca girmek · aralıklara işleyerek içeri girmek · hoş kokulu maddeyi cilde ve saç diplerine sürmek · mideye iyi gelen yiyecek veya içecek · koçun dişiye çiftleşmek üzere girmesi · hançer ya da mızrağı fark ettirmeden sokmak · zırha geçirilmiş perçinler · sözün insanlardan gizli kalmaması
  غللت الشيء في الشيء إذا أثبته فيه كأنه غرزته (maqayis)؛ غله فانغل أي أدخله فدخل (sihah)؛ غل أيضا دخل (sihah)؛ غل في الشيء وانغل وتغلغل فيه إذا دخل فيه (tahdhib)؛ تدرع الشيء وتوسطه (mufradat)؛ تغللت بالغالية وكل شيء ألصقته بجلدك وأصول شعرك (tahdhib)؛ نعم غلول الشيخ هذا يعني الطعام الذي يدخله جوفه (sihah;tahdhib)؛ نعم الغلول شراب شربته أو طعام إذا وافقني (tahdhib)؛ منها ما يغل يعني من الكباش أي يدخل قضيبه (jamhara;sihah)؛ غله له أي دسه له وهو لا يشعر به (tahdhib)؛ غلائل الدروع مساميرها المدخلة فيها (tahdhib)؛ لا يذهب كلامك غللا أي لا ينبغي أن ينطوي عن الناس (tahdhib)

## ب ر ر (root_000104): 76:5 ٱلْأَبْرَارَ

- **B001** sözü ve işi doğrulukla yerine getirme — sözünde ya da işinde doğru olmak · yeminine sadık kalmak, yeminini bozmamak · yemini doğruluk üzere gerçekleştirmek ya da yemin edenin isteğini yerine getirmek · kabul edilmiş, günaha bulaşmamış hac · şaibesiz, yalansız ve hilesiz satış
  برت يمينه أي صدقت وأبرها أمضاها على الصدق (maqayis;ayn;sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ حج مبرور أي مقبول أو لا يخالطه مأثم (maqayis;ayn;sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ البيع المبرور الذي لا شبهة فيه ولا كذب ولا خيانة (tahdhib)
- **B002** inanç ve davranışta geniş kapsamlı iyilik — doğruluk, iyilik, sakınma ve her türlü iyi işi yapma · yaratana gönüllü biçimde uymak · çok şefkatli, merhametli, nazik ve cömert · iyilikte ileri giden doğru ve iyi kimseler
  البر الصلاح والخير والتقى وفعل كل خير (tahdhib;mufradat)؛ يبر ربه أو خالقه أي يطيعه (maqayis;ayn;sihah;mufradat)؛ البر متضمن للاعتقاد والأعمال (mufradat)
- **B003** ana babaya ve yakınlara sevgiyle iyilik etme — ana babaya geniş ölçüde iyilik etmek, onlara kötü davranmamak · yakınlarla bağı sürdürmek ve onlara iyilik etmek
  هو يبر ذا قرابته وأصله الصدق في المحبة (maqayis)؛ البر البار بذوي قرابته (ayn)؛ بررت والدي والبر خلاف العقوق (sihah)؛ بر رحمه إذا وصله وبروا الآباء والأبناء (tahdhib)؛ بر الوالدين التوسع في الإحسان إليهما وضده العقوق (mufradat)
- **B004** gürültülü seslenme ve gevezelik — koyunu sürme veya yeme çağırma sesi · dille çıkarılan gürültü, çok veya öfkeli konuşma · yararsız yere çok konuşan kimse · neyi neyden ayıramamak; açıklaması kaynaklara göre değişen söz
  البر الصوت بها إذا سيقت ودعاء الغنم إلى العلف (maqayis;tahdhib)؛ البربرة كثرة الكلام والجلبة باللسان أو الصوت (maqayis;ayn;sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ لا يعرف هرا من بر (maqayis;sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)
- **B005** denizin karşıtı olan kara — denizin karşıtı olan kara · çöl, ıssız kır veya açık arazi · karaya çıkmak veya karadan yolculuk etmek

  أصل صحيح يدل على قوة في الشيء وصحة (maqayis)؛ أملك عجينه قوي عجنه وشده (maqayis)؛ ملكت العجين إذا شددت عجنه (sihah)؛ ملك النبعة صلبها (sihah)؛ العجين إذا كان متماسكا متينا مملوك ومملك (tahdhib)؛ حائط ليس له ملاك أي تماسك (mufradat)

- **B003** hükümdarlık ve kamusal egemenlik — hükümdar · hükümdar; egemen yönetici · hükümranlık; kamusal egemenlik · ilahi mutlak hükümranlık · hükümdarın yönetim alanı ve ülkesi · birini başlarına hükümdar yapmak
  والاسم الملك لأن يده فيه قوية صحيحة (maqayis)؛ الملك لله المالك المليك (ayn)؛ الملكوت ملك الله وملكوت الله سلطانه (ayn)؛ الملكوت من الملك (sihah)؛ المملكة سلطان الملك في رعيته (ayn;tahdhib)؛ له ملكوت العراق وعزه وسلطانه وملكه (tahdhib)؛ الملك هو المتصرف بالأمر والنهي في الجمهور (mufradat)؛ ملك القوم فلانا وأملكوه على أنفسهم أي صيروه ملكا (tahdhib)
- **B004** evlilik akdi kurma — evlilik akdi; evlendirme · kadınla evlenmek
  كنا في إملاك فلان أي أملكناه امرأته (maqayis)؛ الإملاك التزويج قد أملكوه وملكوه أي زوجوه (ayn)؛ ملكت المرأة تزوجتها (sihah)؛ أملكنا فلانا فلانة إذا زوجناه إياها (sihah)؛ شهدنا إملاك فلان وملاكه وملاكه (tahdhib)؛ الملاك التزويج وأملكوه زوجوه (mufradat)

- **B006** yolun veya yerin orta ya da ana kesimi — yolun ortası veya ana kesimi · vadinin sınırı veya orta kesimi · yerleşimin ortası veya büyük kesimi

  والملك الماء يكون مع المسافر لأنه إذا كان معه ملك أمره (maqayis)؛ الماء ملك أمر أي يقوم به الأمر (sihah)؛ الماء ملك أمره (tahdhib)؛ الماء ملاك الأشياء يضرب للشيء الذي به كمال الأمر (tahdhib)؛ ماله ملك ولا نقر أي ما له ماء (tahdhib)؛ مياهنا ملوكنا ومات فلان عن ملوك كثيرة (tahdhib)

- **B009** ilahi haberci varlık — 
  الملك واحد الملائكة إنما هو تخفيف الملأك والأصل مألك (ayn)؛ مألك من الألوك وهو الرسالة (ayn)؛ الملك من الملائكة واحد وجمع (sihah)؛ أصله مألك بتقديم الهمزة من الألوك وهي الرسالة (sihah)؛ الملك واحد الملائكة إنما هو تخفيف الملأك وهو مفعل من الألوك (tahdhib)

## ك ب ر (root_001281): 76:20 كَبِيرًا

- **B001** küçüğün karşıtı olan büyüklük — büyük · pek büyük · daha büyük veya en büyük
  أصل صحيح يدل على خلاف الصغر (maqayis)؛ كبر كل شيء عظمه (ayn)؛ الكبر ضد الصغر (jamhara)؛ كبر بالضم يكبر أي عظم فهو كبير وكبار (sihah)؛ الكبير والصغير من الأسماء المتضايفة (mufradat)
- **B002** bir işin ana payı ve başlıca yükü — işin büyük bölümü veya ağır yükü · onun işinin en önemli bölümü

- **B003** gözünde büyütüp hayrete düşmek — onu gözünde büyüttü ve ona hayret etti · onu gözlerinde büyüttüler
  أكبرت الشيء استعظمته (maqayis)؛ أكبرت الشيء أكبره إكبارا إذا عظم في صدرك وعجبت منه (jamhara)؛ أكبرت الشيء استعظمته (sihah)؛ أكبرنه أعظمنه (tahdhib)؛ أكبرت الشيء رأيته كبيرا (mufradat)
- **B004** yaşlanma ve zamanla eskime — adam yaşlandı · yaşlılık veya eskilik hali
  ومن الباب الكبر وهو الهرم (maqayis)؛ الكبرة السن يقال علته كبرة (ayn)؛ بلغ فلان الكبر في السن (jamhara)؛ الكبر في السن وقد كبر الرجل أي أسن (sihah)؛ الكبر مصدر الكبير في السن من الناس والدواب (tahdhib)؛ يقال فلان كبير أي مسن (mufradat)؛ السهم والنصل العتيق الذي أفسده الوسخ قد علته كبرة (ayn)؛ للسيف والنصل العتيق الذي قدم علته كبرة (tahdhib)
- **B005** saygınlık ve önderlikte yüksek konum — kuşaktan kuşağa soylu ve saygın biçimde · onların başı veya en bilgilisi · sizin öğreticiniz veya başınız · önder veya en büyük ata
  الرفعة في الشرف (ayn)؛ ورثوا المجد كابرا عن كابر (maqayis;jamhara;sihah;tahdhib;mufradat)؛ كبيرهم أعلمهم كأنه كان رئيسهم (tahdhib)؛ إنه لكبيركم أي رئيسكم (mufradat)؛ الكابر السيد والكابر الجد الأكبر (tahdhib)
- **B006** ululuk ve kendini üstün görme — büyüklük taslama ve kendini üstün görme · ululuk ve boyun eğmeme; Tanrı'ya özgü yücelik · büyüklendi ve kendini üstün gösterdi · gerçeği inatla reddedip büyüklük tasladı
  الكبر العظمة وكذلك الكبرياء (maqayis)؛ الكبرياء اسم للتكبر والعظمة (ayn)؛ تكبر إذا تعظم (jamhara)؛ الكبر بالكسر العظمة وكذلك الكبرياء (sihah)؛ يتكبرون أي يرون أنهم أفضل الخلق (tahdhib)؛ الكبر الحالة التي يتخصص بها الإنسان من إعجابه بنفسه (mufradat)
- **B007** ağır cezalık büyük günah — ağır cezalık büyük günah · ağır cezalık büyük günahlar
  الكبر الإثم الكبير من الكبيرة (ayn)؛ الكبيرة من الذنوب والجمع كبائر (jamhara)؛ كبيرة من الكبائر يعني الذنوب (ayn)؛ الكبيرة متعارفة في كل ذنب تعظم عقوبته (mufradat)؛ إثم كبير (mufradat)
- **B008** soy yakınlığı veya aile içi doğum sırası — soyda en yakın olan veya en büyük evlat · babasının son çocuğu; başka aktarımda en büyük çocuğu
  الولاء للكبر يراد به أقعد القوم في النسب (maqayis)؛ الكبر أكبر ولد الرجل (ayn)؛ فلان كبرة ولد أبويه إذا كان آخرهم (sihah)؛ كبرة ولد أبيه بمعنى عجزة أي آخرهم (tahdhib)؛ هو صغرة ولد أبيه وكبرتهم أي أكبرهم (tahdhib)
- **B009** Tanrı'yı en büyük diye yüceltme — Tanrı'yı en büyük diye yüceltme · Tanrı en büyüktür
  التكبير في الصلاة وغيرها تفعيل من قولهم الله أكبر (jamhara)؛ التكبير التعظيم (sihah)؛ قول المصلي الله أكبر وكذلك قول المؤذن (tahdhib)؛ التكبير يقال لتعظيم الله تعالى بقولهم الله أكبر (mufradat)
- **B010** bir işin birine ağır ve güç gelmesi [kalıp] — bize çok ağır ve güç geldi
  إذا أردت الأمر العظيم قلت كبر علينا كبارة (ayn)؛ فإذا أردت الأمر العظيم قلت كبر علينا كبارة (sihah)؛ كبر الأمر يكبر كبارة (tahdhib)؛ تستعمل الكبيرة فيما يشق ويصعب (mufradat)؛ كبر على المشركين ما تدعوهم إليه (mufradat)
- **B011** üstünlük yarışına girip yenmek [kalıp] — benimle üstünlük yarışına girdi, ben de onu yendim
  كابرني فكبرته أي غلبته (ayn)
- **B012** tek yüzlü davul — tek yüzlü davul
  الكبر طبل له وجه (ayn)؛ الكبر الطبل الذي له وجه واحد (tahdhib)؛ الكبر الطبل وجمعه كبار (tahdhib)
- **B013** günün yükseldiği vakit [kalıp] — günün yükseldiği vakit

- **B001** yukarı yükselme ve yüksekte olma — yukarı olma; aşağı karşıtı yükseklik · bir yerde yükselmek · günün yükselmesi · yükselip uzaklaşmak

  أسفل الشيء وأعلاه (maqayis)؛ جئتك من أعلى ومن علا ومن عال ومن عل (maqayis)؛ علو الدار نقيض سفلها (sihah)؛ علاوة الريح وسفالتها (sihah;mufradat)؛ علاوة الشيء أعلاه (mufradat)

  العلياء رأس كل جبل أو شرف (maqayis)؛ العالية من محال العرب من الحجاز (maqayis)؛ العلية غرفة (maqayis;sihah)؛ العلياء كل مكان مشرف (sihah)؛ لفي عليين (maqayis;mufradat)؛ العلية اسما للغرفة (mufradat)

  العلاوة ما يحمل على البعير بعد تمام الوقر (maqayis)؛ رأس الرجل وعنقه علاوة (maqayis)؛ علوان الكتاب من العلو لأنه أول الكتاب وأعلاه (maqayis)؛ العلاوة ما عليت به على البعير بعد تمام الوقر (sihah)؛ علاوة الشيء أعلاه (mufradat)

  العلاة وهي السندان (maqayis)؛ العلاة حجر يجعل عليه الأقط والعلاة السندان (sihah)؛ عالية الرمح ما دخل في السنان إلى ثلثه (sihah)؛ المعلى السابع من القداح (maqayis;sihah;mufradat)؛ المعلي الذي يمد الدلو (maqayis)

- **B011** bedensel halden kurtulup esenleşme [kalıp] — lohusalıktan temizlenip esenliğe kavuşmak · hastalıktan kurtulmak

- **B012** ilgeç ve kalıplaşmış görev sözü — üzerinde, üzerine veya benzeri ilgeç görevi · şunu al · bana şunu ver · onun yanından veya üstünden

## ث و ب (root_000209): 76:21 ثِيَابُ

- **B001** önceki yere ya da duruma geri dönme — gittikten sonra geri dönmek; önceki yerine veya durumuna gelmek · insanların bir araya gelip toplanması · çekilen suyun geri gelmesi veya kabın yeniden dolması · insanların tekrar tekrar döndüğü yer · kuyu ağzında su çekenin durduğu yer veya suyun geri ulaştığı düzey · havuzda suyun geri dönüp toplandığı bölüm · birbirlerine doğru gelerek toplanan insan topluluğu · arıların geri döndüğü şey olarak bal
  الثاء والواو والباء قياس صحيح من أصل واحد وهو العود والرجوع (maqayis)؛ ثاب يثوب إذا رجع (maqayis)؛ وثاب يثوب ثوبا وثؤوبا إذا رجع وكل راجع ثائب (jamhara)؛ ثاب الرجل يثوب ثوبا وثوبانا رجع بعد ذهابه وثاب الناس اجتمعوا وجاءوا (sihah)؛ أصل الثوب رجوع الشيء إلى حالته الأولى (mufradat)؛ المثابة المكان يثوب إليه الناس (maqayis;sihah;mufradat)؛ ثاب الماء إذا بلغ إلى حاله الأولى بعد ما يستقى (jamhara)؛ ثبة الحوض ما يثوب إليه الماء (mufradat)

(source: latent_activation/network/v3/reviews/s076/reader_a_pilot.md)

# s076 Semantic Channel Discovery

## Parent Channels

### 1. P1 - From Unmarked Duration to a Perceptible Person
- Semantic invariant: An initially unregistered entity crosses a temporal threshold, becomes present, and enters human perception and social memory.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:1-2 anchors duration, non-thinghood, creation, humanity, and mention.
- Surprising reach: Time is not merely a backdrop: its lexical branches arrive, overpower, and bring down events, while humanity becomes an image in the eye and a subject of speech, record, and reputation.

#### Subchannel A. Duration Delivers an Event into Existence
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Extended time reaches its appointed moment and carries an entity from non-description into occurrence.
- Active motifs: arriving event (quranic:root_000009:B001/m01); destructive arrival (quranic:root_000009:B011/m01); appointed time (quranic:root_000382:B001/m01); time of death or calamity (quranic:root_000382:B002/m01); overpowering duration (quranic:root_000494:B001/m01); extended age (quranic:root_000494:B002/m01); descending vicissitude (quranic:root_000494:B004/m01); occurrence in time (quranic:root_001332:B001/m01); knowable thing (quranic:root_000831:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:1 (ء ت ي, ح ي ن, د ه ر, ك و ن, ش ي ء)
- Synthesis: The scene treats the opening interval as an active delivery mechanism. Duration stretches and overpowers, a critical time arrives, and what had not counted as a reportable thing becomes an occurrence that can be known and predicated. The destructive senses of arrival and time keep emergence under the pressure of mortality rather than presenting existence as neutral persistence.

#### Subchannel B. The Stranger Becomes Visible, Named, and Socially Present
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A previously unrecognized entrant is detected, given human presence, and installed in memory and social speech.
- Active motifs: stranger entering another people (quranic:root_000009:B006/m01); human presence opposed to wild absence (quranic:root_000059:B001/m01); detection by sight, sound, or feeling (quranic:root_000059:B002/m01); companionship removing estrangement (quranic:root_000059:B003/m01); human image in the pupil (quranic:root_000059:B005/m01); remembered after absence (quranic:root_000516:B003/m01); spoken mention (quranic:root_000516:B004/m01); public repute (quranic:root_000516:B007/m01); documentary record (quranic:root_000516:B008/m01); reminder that restores presence (quranic:root_000516:B009/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:1 (ء ت ي, ء ن س, ذ ك ر); 76:2 (ء ن س)
- Synthesis: The human is materialized as an entrant into a populated field: first detectable, then companionable, then literally imaged in another eye. Mention completes that admission by moving through inward recollection, speech, public reputation, and written record. “Mentioned” thus marks social and epistemic registration, not only verbal description.

### 2. P1 - Fabricating a Mixed, Jointed Perceiver
- Semantic invariant: Human creation is a staged fabrication in which mixed fluid becomes a measured, articulated body equipped to receive and answer evidence.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:2 names creation from mixed fluid, testing, hearing, and sight.
- Surprising reach: The sensory organs cast lexical shadows as seams, shields, handles, fetters, and a brain, making perception both bodily equipment and an accountability interface.

#### Subchannel A. Fluid, Mixture, and Interwoven Strands
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Generative fluid is blended from several inputs and figured as strands entering one another before form is fixed.
- Active motifs: clear generative water (quranic:root_001518:B002/m01); dripping and extended flow (quranic:root_001518:B003/m01); general intermixture (quranic:root_001426:B001/m01); composite reproductive drop (quranic:root_001426:B002/m01); interwoven yarns (quranic:root_001426:B004/m01); bodily temperament composed from humors (quranic:root_001417:B002/m01); measured creation (quranic:root_000434:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2 (ن ط ف, م ش ج, خ ل ق); 76:5 (م ز ج)
- Synthesis: The drop is not an undifferentiated liquid. It is a composite material whose parts are mixed like interpenetrating yarns and whose bodily constitution is itself a “mixture.” Measurement precedes fabrication, so the process moves from fluid plurality to a proportioned substrate rather than from formlessness to an arbitrary shape.

#### Subchannel B. Measured Form, Joints, Seams, and Ribs
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A maker estimates dimensions, produces a form, joins its members, and closes its surfaces.
- Active motifs: fabrication and bringing into being (quranic:root_000248:B001/m01); transformation into a state (quranic:root_000248:B002/m01); prior measurement (quranic:root_000434:B001/m01); creative production (quranic:root_000434:B002/m01); balanced visible form (quranic:root_000434:B003/m01); smooth finished surface (quranic:root_000434:B008/m01); firm joints and articulated constitution (quranic:root_000030:B002/m01); ribs of the chest (quranic:root_000266:B016/m01); joining two hides at a thick edge (quranic:root_000121:B006/m01); sequentially connected formation (quranic:root_000934:B014/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2 (ج ع ل, خ ل ق, ب ص ر); 76:8 (ء س ر); 76:9 (ط ع م); 76:12 (ج ن ن)
- Synthesis: These motifs form a workshop scene: estimate, make, transform, articulate, seam, and finish. The otherwise abstract “creation” is pressured into a built body with fastened joints, enclosing ribs, and joined surfaces. The later prisoner-root contributes a materially different but coherent sense: the same lexical field that denotes captivity also denotes the body’s own binding together.

#### Subchannel C. Sensory Equipment Becomes an Accountability Interface
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Ear, brain, eye, and inward discernment receive evidence; their tool-senses expose the equipment-like character of perception.
- Active motifs: hearing by the ear (quranic:root_000741:B001/m01); ear and auditory aperture (quranic:root_000741:B002/m01); understanding that issues in compliance (quranic:root_000741:B003/m01); brain as “mother of hearing” (quranic:root_000741:B016/m01); sight by the eye (quranic:root_000121:B001/m01); inward insight and self-witness (quranic:root_000121:B002/m01); bucket handle that balances a load (quranic:root_000741:B008/m01); auditory fetter (quranic:root_000741:B009/m01); shield or armor of sight (quranic:root_000121:B005/m01); trial revealing quality (quranic:root_000153:B002/m01); trial revealing quality (quranic:root_000154:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2 (س م ع, ب ص ر, ب ل و); unavailable for ب ل ي
- Synthesis: Hearing and sight are first anatomical and cognitive capacities, but their lexical extensions make them load-bearing and juridical: the ear can be a balancing handle or a fetter, while sight can be protective armor. Trial activates the equipment by making hidden quality visible. The result is not a neutral sensorium but a fitted interface through which understanding may become obedience and testimony against the self.

### 3. P1 - A Guided Route with Divergent Cultivation
- Semantic invariant: Guidance supplies direction and a traversable route; the recipient’s response either exposes growth or covers what was given.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:3 presents guidance to the path followed by gratitude or ingratitude.
- Surprising reach: The response pair becomes an agricultural operation: gratitude makes a small input visibly flourish, while unbelief covers seed and encloses fruit.

#### Subchannel A. Gentle Pathfinding and Correct Orientation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A leader goes ahead, identifies a route and its destination, and directs travelers toward the sound face of the matter.
- Active motifs: gentle guidance to road and truth (quranic:root_001583:B001/m01); bearing, direction, and manner (quranic:root_001583:B002/m01); guide or vanguard at the head (quranic:root_001583:B003/m01); calm, well-composed gait (quranic:root_001583:B010/m01); extended traversable road (quranic:root_000672:B001/m01); road users and travelers (quranic:root_000672:B002/m01); destination and direction (quranic:root_001630:B002/m01); sound face of an affair (quranic:root_001630:B008/m01); scouted pasture or sought object (quranic:root_000610:B003/m01); gentleness and measured pace (quranic:root_000610:B005/m01); traveled road and terminal limit (quranic:root_000009:B010/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:1 (ء ت ي); 76:3 (ه د ي, س ب ل); 76:9 (و ج ه, ر و د)
- Synthesis: Guidance appears as a practical navigation scene rather than an abstract transfer of information. A vanguard moves ahead, scouts what is needed, establishes direction, and conducts travelers with a calm pace toward a terminus. The face-root adds evaluative orientation: the route is also the sound way of handling the affair.

#### Subchannel B. Gratitude Germinates; Ingratitude Covers
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A received input is either made productive and visible or buried beneath a cover.
- Active motifs: recognition and manifestation of favor (quranic:root_000810:B001/m01); little input yielding visible growth (quranic:root_000810:B002/m01); fullness and abundant milk (quranic:root_000810:B003/m01); emergence of tender shoots (quranic:root_000810:B004/m01); covering and concealment (quranic:root_001307:B001/m01); concealment of a favor (quranic:root_001307:B004/m01); seed covered with soil (quranic:root_001307:B008/m01); sheath enclosing fruit (quranic:root_001307:B010/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:3 (ش ك ر, ك ف ر); 76:4-5 (ك ف ر); 76:9 (ش ك ر)
- Synthesis: The ethical alternatives are materialized through opposite treatments of a gift. Gratitude lets a slight ration or slight rain show itself as fatness, milk, and shoots; ingratitude covers, buries, and seals. The seed-covering sense is not simply negative, but under the surface contrast it pressures the question of whether covering becomes germination or concealment without acknowledgment.

### 4. P1 - Obligation Completed under an Approaching Event
- Semantic invariant: A self-assumed obligation is carried to completion while warning converts the future into a presently governing event.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:7 links fulfillment of vows with fear of a day whose evil spreads.
- Surprising reach: Vow, debt, appointment, and compensation converge into a settlement scene, while the feared day behaves like a rapidly dispersing material.

#### Subchannel A. Vow, Appointment, and Settlement
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person binds the self, meets the appointed obligation, and closes the account without deficiency.
- Active motifs: self-imposed obligation (quranic:root_001488:B002/m01); obligatory compensation for injury (quranic:root_001488:B003/m01); completion without deficiency (quranic:root_001669:B001/m01); arrival at an appointment (quranic:root_001669:B004/m01); recompense corresponding to action (quranic:root_000244:B001/m01); debt demanded and collected (quranic:root_000244:B003/m01); truthful oath and transaction (quranic:root_000104:B001/m01); covenant and pact (quranic:root_000532:B011/m01); excuse or oath displayed to remove blame (quranic:root_000153:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2 (ب ل و); 76:5 (ب ر ر); 76:7 (ن ذ ر, و ف ي); 76:9, 76:12 (ج ز ي); 76:10 (ر ب ب)
- Synthesis: Fulfillment is rendered as the exact closing of an obligation. A vow is a voluntarily incurred liability; fulfillment brings it whole to its appointment; recompense and debt collection supply the settlement mechanics. Truthful oath and disclosed excuse define the evidentiary edge of the scene: nothing remains deficient, hidden, or contestable.

#### Subchannel B. Warning Makes the Future Calamity Present
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Warning awakens fear before a severe event arrives and disperses its harm in every direction.
- Active motifs: warning that awakens caution (quranic:root_001488:B001/m01); fear anticipating harm (quranic:root_000447:B001/m01); instilling fear to induce precaution (quranic:root_000447:B002/m01); fear visibly appearing on a person (quranic:root_000447:B005/m01); great event embodied as a day (quranic:root_001700:B003/m01); evil and injury (quranic:root_000787:B001/m01); sparks cast from fire (quranic:root_000787:B003/m01); light matter spreading and flying apart (quranic:root_000962:B002/m01); calamity arriving upon a person (quranic:root_000009:B011/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:1 (ء ت ي); 76:7 (ن ذ ر, خ و ف, ي و م, ش ر ر, ط ي ر); 76:10 (خ و ف, ي و م); 76:11 (ش ر ر, ي و م)
- Synthesis: Warning pulls a future day into present conduct. Fear first anticipates and then becomes visible; the day is no longer a date but a great occurrence whose evil behaves like sparks or light particles scattering outward. That motion explains why fulfilled obligation is urgent: the event’s reach is already imagined as uncontainable.

### 5. P1 - Passage Bound Shut or Opened into Flow
- Semantic invariant: The pericope contrasts apparatus that obstructs bodily passage with channels that open, conduct, mix, and serve liquid.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:4-6 moves from chains, collars, and blaze to a mixed cup and a spring made to gush.
- Surprising reach: The chain-root itself carries smooth, easy drinking, so the punitive linkage is lexically reversed into an unimpeded current.

#### Subchannel A. Prepared Hardware of Confinement and Internal Heat
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Equipment is made ready, linked around the body, fixed into openings, and heated into an inward thirst.
- Active motifs: ready equipment (quranic:root_000978:B001/m01); connected iron rings (quranic:root_000731:B002/m01); iron collar binding a limb or neck (quranic:root_001102:B006/m01); fixed insertion into interstices (quranic:root_001102:B001/m01); binding and captivity (quranic:root_000030:B001/m01); fetter called a “hearing” (quranic:root_000741:B009/m01); rope placed around the neck (quranic:root_000783:B008/m01); kindled blaze (quranic:root_000708:B001/m01); subterranean oven (quranic:root_000708:B009/m01); flying sparks (quranic:root_000787:B003/m01); rabid heat and thirst (quranic:root_000708:B004/m01); heat penetrating the interior (quranic:root_001102:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2 (س م ع); 76:4 (ع ت د, س ل س ل, غ ل ل, س ع ر); 76:5-6 (ش ر ب); 76:7, 76:11 (ش ر ر); 76:8 (ء س ر)
- Synthesis: Confinement is assembled as a mechanism: linked rings, collar, rope, and inserted fastenings close movement around the neck and limbs. Fire then migrates inward as unquenched thirst and agitation. The sensory fetter and drinking-rope senses sharpen the reversal: equipment associated elsewhere with hearing and drinking is converted here into obstruction.

#### Subchannel B. Smooth Drink through an Opened Source
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A source opens, liquid travels through a controlled course, is mixed in a cup, and reaches the drinker without resistance.
- Active motifs: smooth, sweet, easy-flowing drink (quranic:root_000731:B001/m01); flowing spring (quranic:root_001069:B006/m01); wide fissure releasing water (quranic:root_001132:B001/m01); taking liquid (quranic:root_000783:B001/m01); drinking place and vessel (quranic:root_000783:B004/m01); cup with its contents (quranic:root_001277:B001/m01); mixing one liquid into another (quranic:root_001417:B001/m01); camphor perfume or spring (quranic:root_001307:B011/m01); watercourse directed to a basin (quranic:root_000009:B004/m01); aperture in a waterskin (quranic:root_001069:B007/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:1 (ء ت ي); 76:4 (س ل س ل); 76:5 (ش ر ب, ك ء س, م ز ج, ك ف ر); 76:6 (ع ي ن, ش ر ب, ف ج ر)
- Synthesis: Release is as engineered as confinement. A fissure opens the source, a course conducts it, an aperture regulates it, and mixing prepares the cup. The smooth-drink branch of the chain-root turns linked continuity from an iron restraint into liquid continuity, providing a precise role reversal rather than a loose contrast between punishment and reward.

### 6. P1 - Cultivation from Incoming Water to Edible Yield
- Semantic invariant: Water arrives, is routed into ground, activates covered seed, and culminates in ripened food and a sheltering garden.
- Surface relation: indirect; 76:2 and 76:5-9 supply fluid, mixture, drink, and feeding, while 76:12 supplies the garden.
- Surprising reach: Ethical response and eschatological reward are materialized as a full cultivation cycle rather than as isolated images of drink, food, and garden.

#### Subchannel A. Rain, Channels, and Saturated Ground
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water arrives from elsewhere, falls, gathers, splits channels, and leaves land green and saturated.
- Active motifs: incoming flood from another country (quranic:root_000009:B005/m01); abundant water and yield (quranic:root_000009:B007/m01); rain-bearing eye of cloud (quranic:root_001069:B010/m01); layered rain cloud (quranic:root_000532:B008/m01); abundant collected water (quranic:root_000532:B013/m01); rain suspended between cloud and earth (quranic:root_000672:B005/m01); continuous dripping and rain (quranic:root_001518:B003/m01); fissured water channels (quranic:root_001132:B001/m01); soft irrigated green land (quranic:root_000783:B014/m01); water and plants among trees (quranic:root_001102:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:1 (ء ت ي); 76:2 (ن ط ف); 76:3 (س ب ل); 76:4 (غ ل ل); 76:5-6 (ش ر ب); 76:6 (ع ي ن, ف ج ر); 76:10 (ر ب ب)
- Synthesis: Hydrology begins before the garden appears. Water comes from beyond the locality, hangs as rain, gathers in quantity, and is opened into channels that saturate soil and woodland. The scene gives the later garden a material prehistory and makes the act of “gushing” the irrigation hinge of a broader ecology.

#### Subchannel B. Covered Grain Emerges as Shoots and Palms
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Seed is covered, watered, and driven upward into ears, tender shoots, and young palms.
- Active motifs: wheat and grain (quranic:root_000104:B006/m01); seed capable of germination (quranic:root_000286:B001/m01); burial of seed in soil (quranic:root_001307:B008/m01); fruit sheath (quranic:root_001307:B010/m01); extended ear of grain (quranic:root_000672:B008/m01); tender shoot emerging (quranic:root_000810:B004/m01); small palms (quranic:root_000248:B006/m01); small palms (quranic:root_000831:B008/m01); small palms (quranic:root_000832:B006/m01); dense rising vegetation (quranic:root_000266:B011/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:1 (ش ي ء); 76:2 (ج ع ل); 76:3, 76:9 (ش ك ر); 76:3-5 (ك ف ر); 76:3 (س ب ل); 76:5 (ب ر ر); 76:8 (ح ب ب); 76:12 (ج ن ن)
- Synthesis: The crop scene is exact in its operations: grain is put under cover, an ear extends, tender growth appears, and the young palm rises into dense vegetation. The two small-palm motifs converge on the same stage of growth, reinforcing the transition from shoot to young tree.

#### Subchannel C. Ripening, Harvest, and Nourishment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Color changes mark ripeness, fruit is recognized by taste, and produce becomes food within a sheltering orchard.
- Active motifs: grain changing from green to yellow (quranic:root_001417:B004/m01); fruit reaching flavor and maturity (quranic:root_000934:B005/m01); crop and palm yield (quranic:root_000009:B007/m01); orchard screened by trees (quranic:root_000266:B003/m01); tamarind-like sour fruit (quranic:root_000840:B009/m01); tender greens eaten raw (quranic:root_000306:B013/m01); fullness and abundant milk (quranic:root_000810:B003/m01); verdant radiance of a plant (quranic:root_001516:B001/m01); feeding another (quranic:root_000934:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:1 (ء ت ي); 76:3, 76:9 (ش ك ر); 76:5 (م ز ج); 76:8-9 (ط ع م); 76:11 (ن ض ر); 76:12 (ص ب ر, ج ن ن, ح ر ر)
- Synthesis: Ripeness is shown as a perceptible transition: color shifts, taste develops, and produce moves into feeding. Garden and radiance close the process by joining edible yield to protected flourishing. The channel thus reads the pericope’s nourishment as the end of cultivation, not as disconnected abundance.

### 7. P1 - Provision Reattaches the Socially Severed
- Semantic invariant: Feeding repairs broken support relations while refusing to turn aid into debt, praise, or exchange.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:8-9 names the poor person, orphan, captive, feeding, love, divine orientation, and refusal of recompense or thanks.
- Surprising reach: A botanical graft that accepts an alien branch provides a concrete mechanism for restoring those cut off from household, guardian, or freedom.

#### Subchannel A. From Isolation and Captivity Back toward a Household
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: People deprived of means, guardian, or liberty are met by roles associated with home, kin, and foster care.
- Active motifs: poverty and humbled weakness (quranic:root_000726:B006/m01); dwelling and home (quranic:root_000726:B002/m01); household inhabitants (quranic:root_000726:B003/m01); child cut off from a guardian (quranic:root_001692:B001/m01); unique isolated object (quranic:root_001692:B002/m01); delayed beneficence (quranic:root_001692:B004/m01); binding and captivity (quranic:root_000030:B001/m01); family from whom one draws strength (quranic:root_000030:B003/m01); kinship kindness against severance (quranic:root_000104:B003/m01); foster child and caregiver (quranic:root_000532:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:5 (ب ر ر); 76:8 (س ك ن, ي ت م, ء س ر); 76:10 (ر ب ب)
- Synthesis: The vulnerable figures share a structural loss: means, guardian, household, or bodily freedom. The lexical field supplies the absent counterparts—home, household, strong kin group, and foster care—so feeding becomes a limited but concrete act of social reattachment rather than generic charity.

#### Subchannel B. Food as Provision and Graft
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Food is transferred to another and functions like a graft that lets a foreign branch join a sustaining stock.
- Active motifs: feeding another and answering a request for food (quranic:root_000934:B002/m01); provision and livelihood (quranic:root_000934:B004/m01); graft accepting a branch from another tree (quranic:root_000934:B010/m01); provision that keeps a group in place (quranic:root_000726:B010/m01); enduring love (quranic:root_000286:B002/m01); giving the whole self and concern (quranic:root_000787:B007/m01); generosity bursting outward (quranic:root_001132:B005/m01); wealth spent in feeding and watering (quranic:root_000783:B012/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:5-6 (ش ر ب); 76:6 (ف ج ر); 76:7, 76:11 (ش ر ر); 76:8 (ح ب ب, س ك ن, ط ع م); 76:9 (ط ع م)
- Synthesis: Provision is both substance and joining operation. Food and water sustain residence; love supplies motive; the graft sense gives the social frame a precise mechanism, in which what was external is accepted into a living support system. The bursting-generosity and whole-self motifs keep the transfer from shrinking into a minimal ration.

#### Subchannel C. A Gift outside the Exchange Ledger
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Intention is oriented toward God while compensation, debt collection, public praise, and reciprocal thanks are explicitly declined.
- Active motifs: intention and volition (quranic:root_000610:B001/m01); face and outward orientation (quranic:root_001630:B001/m01); direction and destination (quranic:root_001630:B002/m01); sound management of an affair (quranic:root_001630:B008/m01); worship and the worshipped (quranic:root_000047:B001/m01); corresponding recompense (quranic:root_000244:B001/m01); substitute that discharges another’s liability (quranic:root_000244:B002/m01); debt collection (quranic:root_000244:B003/m01); expressed gratitude (quranic:root_000810:B001/m01); public reputation (quranic:root_000516:B007/m01); fame heard among people (quranic:root_000741:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:1 (ذ ك ر); 76:2 (س م ع); 76:3, 76:9 (ش ك ر); 76:6, 76:9, 76:11 (ء ل ه); 76:9 (ر و د, و ج ه, ج ز ي)
- Synthesis: The act is placed outside ordinary reciprocity. The face supplies orientation, intention supplies inward direction, and worship identifies the addressee; against these stand the familiar ledgers of equivalent return, substituted liability, collected debt, thanks, and reputation. Refusing all of them prevents the beneficiary from becoming a debtor or publicity instrument.

### 8. P1 - The Contracted Face of Calamity and Its Counter-Reception
- Semantic invariant: A severe event marks the face and compresses the body, while protection reverses that pressure into a meeting characterized by brightness and settled joy.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:10-11 moves from fear of a grim, constricting day to protection, radiance, and joy.
- Surprising reach: The day is figured as both a face and a tightened container; its answer is not mere relief but a deliberately staged reception.

#### Subchannel A. A Day that Frowns, Accumulates, and Closes
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Severity becomes facial contraction, gathered pressure, and a vessel drawn tight at its mouth.
- Active motifs: contracted, hostile face (quranic:root_000975:B001/m01); severe repellent day (quranic:root_000975:B002/m01); severe day with spreading evil (quranic:root_001257:B001/m01); accumulation and inward contraction (quranic:root_001257:B002/m01); waterskin tied at the mouth (quranic:root_001257:B003/m01); visible appearance of fear (quranic:root_000447:B005/m01); great calamitous event (quranic:root_001700:B003/m01); face confronting the observer (quranic:root_001630:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:7, 76:10-11 (ي و م); 76:7, 76:10 (خ و ف); 76:9 (و ج ه); 76:10 (ع ب س, ق م ط ر)
- Synthesis: The feared day is personified with more than a frown. Its severity gathers and contracts like material forced inward, then closes like a tightly tied waterskin. Fear appears on the human face before the day arrives, so facial expression becomes the contact surface between anticipated event and present body.

#### Subchannel B. Barrier, Encounter, Brightness, and Repose
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A protective barrier intercepts harm and leads into an encounter that gives visible brightness and inward ease.
- Active motifs: barrier against injury (quranic:root_001677:B001/m01); self placed within protection (quranic:root_001677:B002/m01); evil or harm met and intercepted (quranic:root_000787:B001/m01); meeting two parties face to face (quranic:root_001372:B004/m01); what one encounters of good or evil (quranic:root_001372:B007/m01); radiant beauty of a face (quranic:root_001516:B001/m02); pure gold-like brilliance (quranic:root_001516:B002/m01); inward joy and ease (quranic:root_000697:B010/m01); settled couch or place of repose (quranic:root_000697:B011/m01); protective shield (quranic:root_000266:B008/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:7, 76:11 (ش ر ر); 76:11 (و ق ي, ل ق ي, ن ض ر, س ر ر); 76:12 (ج ن ن)
- Synthesis: Protection first inserts a barrier, then changes the kind of encounter. Instead of meeting evil, the participants are met with brilliance and repose. Radiance externalizes the reversal on the face, while joy and the settled couch locate it inwardly and spatially. The garden-root’s shield sense extends the protection into the next ayah’s reward.

### 9. P1 - Self-Restraint Exchanged for Shelter and Freedom
- Semantic invariant: Voluntary holding under pressure is answered by enclosing protection, cultivated shelter, fine covering, and release from bondage.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:12 names recompense for patience as garden and silk, with protection already established in 76:11.
- Surprising reach: The silk-root also means freedom and purification, so the rewarded garment performs a lexical release from the coercive restraints earlier in the pericope.

#### Subchannel A. Patience as Holding, Endurance, and Bitter Material
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The self is held steady while coercion, blocked escape, hard ground, cold, bitterness, and accumulated layers test that hold.
- Active motifs: holding the self from panic (quranic:root_000840:B001/m01); coercive detention for death or oath (quranic:root_000840:B002/m01); surety and continued accompaniment (quranic:root_000840:B003/m01); severe situation without an exit (quranic:root_000840:B006/m01); hard stone and gravelly ground (quranic:root_000840:B005/m01); severe winter cold (quranic:root_000840:B007/m01); bitter medicinal extract (quranic:root_000840:B008/m01); layered white cloud (quranic:root_000840:B010/m01); food piled in layers (quranic:root_000840:B011/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:12 (ص ب ر)
- Synthesis: Patience is a holding operation with distinct pressures around it. The voluntary act of steadying the self is differentiated from forced detention, then materialized by no-exit severity, hard ground, cold, bitterness, and layers that bear upon one another. Surety adds the social role of remaining answerable and present through the ordeal.

#### Subchannel B. Garden, Shield, Silk, and Emancipation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Recompense converts endurance into a protected, growing enclosure and a garment whose lexical field includes freedom and purity.
- Active motifs: corresponding recompense (quranic:root_000244:B001/m01); concealment and enclosure (quranic:root_000266:B001/m01); tree-screened garden (quranic:root_000266:B003/m01); protective shield (quranic:root_000266:B008/m01); dense rising vegetation (quranic:root_000266:B011/m01); refuge for concealment (quranic:root_000266:B017/m01); fine silk garment (quranic:root_000306:B010/m01); freedom from servitude or captivity (quranic:root_000306:B002/m01); purity from defect (quranic:root_000306:B003/m01); barrier against harm (quranic:root_001677:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:9, 76:12 (ج ز ي); 76:11 (و ق ي); 76:12 (ج ن ن, ح ر ر)
- Synthesis: The garden is simultaneously vegetation, enclosure, shield, and refuge, so reward answers patience with a place that both grows and protects. Silk then becomes more than luxury: within its root it carries emancipation and freedom from defect. The sequence reverses coercive binding into a voluntarily earned covering that signifies release.

### 10. P1 - A Desert Caravan Conducted to Water
- Semantic invariant: Route finding, animal movement, equipment, water allotment, and pasture assemble into a complete travel-and-watering scene.
- Surface relation: indirect; 76:3 supplies route and guidance, while 76:5-6 supplies drinking and a source.
- Surprising reach: The ethical path and paradisal spring are joined by the practical logistics of a caravan whose exhausted mounts, scouts, buckets, and water shares make guidance materially consequential.

#### Subchannel A. Vanguard, Scout, Route, and Mount
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A vanguard leads travelers while a scout searches pasture and mounts advance, slow, or fail.
- Active motifs: vanguard guide (quranic:root_001583:B003/m01); traversable road (quranic:root_000672:B001/m01); road users and travelers (quranic:root_000672:B002/m01); scout seeking pasture (quranic:root_000610:B003/m01); roaming between grazing places (quranic:root_000610:B004/m01); gentle pace (quranic:root_000610:B005/m01); camel forelegs moving in gait (quranic:root_000009:B009/m01); camel stopped by exhaustion or injury (quranic:root_000286:B005/m01); rapid but limited run (quranic:root_000973:B009/m01); mount failing on the road (quranic:root_000973:B011/m01); prepared strong horse (quranic:root_000978:B002/m01); ascent to a lookout (quranic:root_001669:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:1 (ء ت ي); 76:3 (ه د ي, س ب ل); 76:4 (ع ت د); 76:6 (ع ب د); 76:7 (و ف ي); 76:8 (ح ب ب); 76:9 (ر و د)
- Synthesis: The route has participants and failure modes. A guide goes first, a scout searches ahead, travelers follow, and mounts alternate between steady gait, speed, exhaustion, and collapse. The lookout supplies reconnaissance. This is a coherent travel scene in which “guidance” includes the bodily and logistical problem of bringing a group through difficult ground.

#### Subchannel B. Water Share, Source, Bucket, and Carrying Skin
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A traveling group reaches a source, allocates turns, draws water with balanced gear, fills skins, and secures pasture.
- Active motifs: assigned share of water (quranic:root_000783:B002/m01); community of drinkers (quranic:root_000783:B003/m01); watering place and vessel (quranic:root_000783:B004/m01); flowing source (quranic:root_001069:B006/m01); bucket handle or side balancing a load (quranic:root_000741:B008/m01); leather bag of the honey gatherer or water carrier (quranic:root_000447:B006/m01); filling to repletion (quranic:root_000286:B006/m01); pasture and provisions that prevent migration (quranic:root_000726:B010/m01); directed watercourse (quranic:root_000009:B004/m01); clear water from drop to sea (quranic:root_001518:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:1 (ء ت ي); 76:2 (س م ع, ن ط ف); 76:5-6 (ش ر ب); 76:6 (ع ي ن); 76:7, 76:10 (خ و ف); 76:8 (ح ب ب, س ك ن)
- Synthesis: The watering scene completes the caravan: water is not merely found but apportioned, drawn, carried, and made sufficient for residence and grazing. Bucket handle, load-balancing side, and leather bag give the scene tools; filling and pasture give it an outcome. The spring in 76:6 thus reads as the terminus of an organized journey.

### 11. P2 - Pacified Climate, Protective Canopy, and Accessible Harvest
- Semantic invariant: Environmental extremes are neutralized, overhead cover becomes protective, and produce is brought within effortless reach.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:13-14 removes sun and severe cold, lowers shade, and makes fruit clusters tractable.
- Surprising reach: Sun and cold also denote restlessness, hostility, anger, and withholding, so climatic balance extends into pacified affect and unblocked hospitality.

#### Subchannel A. Heat, Cold, Hostility, and Restlessness Stilled
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Reclining occurs in an environment from which thermal and affective extremes have been removed.
- Active motifs: sun and its heat (quranic:root_000818:B001/m01); restive animal refusing its back (quranic:root_000818:B002/m01); overt hostility (quranic:root_000818:B003/m01); withholding and parsimony (quranic:root_000818:B006/m01); severe cold (quranic:root_000645:B001/m01); eye reddened with anger (quranic:root_000645:B002/m01); stellar shimmer (quranic:root_000645:B003/m01); staying in place (quranic:root_000026:B003/m01); wound settling after repair (quranic:root_000026:B005/m01); leaning on a support (quranic:root_001678:B004/m01); cooling and repose (quranic:root_001215:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:13 (ء ر ك, ش م س, ز م ه ر, و ك ء); 76:15-16 (ق ر ر)
- Synthesis: The absence of sun and severe cold creates more than thermal comfort. Their associated senses remove balking, hostility, anger, and stinginess from the social atmosphere. Staying, healing, leaning, and cooling supply the positive state: a body can settle because both climate and antagonistic impulse have been pacified.

#### Subchannel B. Shade Becomes Near Protective Architecture
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An overhead cover approaches the occupants and functions as canopy, guard, and cool reservoir.
- Active motifs: ordinary shade (quranic:root_000966:B001/m01); overhead canopy or cloud (quranic:root_000966:B003/m01); protection and honored shelter (quranic:root_000966:B004/m01); approach signaled by a cast shadow (quranic:root_000966:B005/m01); shaded standing water (quranic:root_000966:B008/m01); nearness and approximation (quranic:root_000493:B001/m01); gentle yielding without humiliation (quranic:root_000519:B002/m01); arak tree (quranic:root_000026:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:13 (ء ر ك); 76:14 (د ن و, ظ ل ل, ذ ل ل)
- Synthesis: Shade is treated as a mobile piece of architecture: it comes near, covers from above, guards, and preserves cool water beneath vegetation. Gentleness replaces coercive lowering, so the environment accommodates the resident without debasing either resident or object.

#### Subchannel C. Fruit Made Ripe, Low, and Collectable
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Produce reaches maturity, hangs low, and can be cut, gathered, or taken without difficult movement.
- Active motifs: making a difficult thing tractable (quranic:root_000519:B003/m01); things running in their proper course (quranic:root_000519:B004/m01); hanging lower edge (quranic:root_000519:B005/m01); cutting fruit from its source (quranic:root_001241:B001/m01); picked fruit and near cluster (quranic:root_001241:B002/m01); arrival of harvest time (quranic:root_001241:B003/m01); fallen gleanings (quranic:root_001241:B004/m01); tender broad-leaf vegetable (quranic:root_001241:B008/m01); ripe pasture and fruit becoming available (quranic:root_000956:B007/m01); taking into possession (quranic:root_000018:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:14 (ذ ل ل, ق ط ف); 76:24 (ط و ع); 76:29 (ء خ ذ)
- Synthesis: Accessibility is defined by operations, not by abundance alone. The cluster is lowered, its harvest time has arrived, it can be cut cleanly, and even gleanings remain available. The later obedience-root adds the latent sense of fruit becoming ready for use, while taking supplies the final transfer to the beneficiary.

### 12. P2 - A Calibrated Hydraulic Hospitality System
- Semantic invariant: Sources, storage, measured containers, circulation, and serving form an integrated water-and-drink infrastructure.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:15-18 and 76:21 present vessels, silver, glass, exact measure, cups, mixture, a named spring, and divine service of a pure drink.
- Surprising reach: Later roots for taking, creation, knowledge, counting, and kingship preserve wells and basins, extending the paradise scene into an estate whose water supply underwrites its governance.

#### Subchannel A. Source, Basin, Well, and Estate Supply
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water rises from a source, is gathered in durable basins and wells, and becomes the resource by which an estate can remain and govern.
- Active motifs: flowing spring (quranic:root_001069:B006/m01); easy, delicious, fast-running water (quranic:root_000730:B002/m01); waterway made available at will (quranic:root_000722:B002/m01); irrigation share and channel (quranic:root_000722:B003/m01); basin retaining water for days (quranic:root_000018:B006/m01); ancient or perennial well (quranic:root_000989:B004/m01); sea or well of abundant water (quranic:root_001040:B005/m01); rock hollow or new well holding rain (quranic:root_000434:B011/m01); water as the means of controlling an encampment (quranic:root_001444:B007/m01); shaded pool (quranic:root_000966:B008/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:14 (ظ ل ل); 76:17, 76:21 (س ق ي); 76:18 (ع ي ن); 76:20 (م ل ك); 76:28 (خ ل ق); 76:29 (ء خ ذ); 76:30 (ع ل م); 76:31 (ع د د); unavailable for س ل س ب ل
- Synthesis: The spring belongs to a larger hydraulic estate. Water is made available, channeled, retained in basins, secured in lasting wells, and treated as the resource that makes settlement and rule possible. This infrastructure gives concrete depth to the effortless abundance of the surface scene.

#### Subchannel B. Silver-Glass Vessels Made to Exact Measure
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Material is separated and shaped into stable, transparent containers whose dimensions match their use.
- Active motifs: vessel or container (quranic:root_000063:B004/m01); silver material (quranic:root_001162:B003/m01); glass bottle (quranic:root_001215:B012/m01); cup without handle (quranic:root_001328:B001/m01); prescribed amount and limit (quranic:root_001205:B001/m01); deliberate design and calculation (quranic:root_001205:B005/m01); fit to a proper middle measure (quranic:root_001205:B006/m01); stability in place (quranic:root_001215:B003/m01); pouring in one movement (quranic:root_001215:B005/m01); broken and separated material (quranic:root_001162:B001/m01); detached fragments (quranic:root_001162:B004/m01); flowing silver-like liquid (quranic:root_001162:B006/m01); tied mouth of a vessel (quranic:root_001678:B001/m01); bottle or well stopper (quranic:root_000840:B018/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:13 (و ك ء); 76:15 (ء ن ي, ف ض ض, ك و ب, ق ر ر); 76:16 (ف ض ض, ق د ر, ق ر ر); 76:24 (ص ب ر)
- Synthesis: The vessels combine apparent material paradoxes through a coherent craft sequence: material separates, fragments can be selected, liquid is contained, the form is stabilized, and capacity is calculated to fit. Tied mouth and stopper complete the container mechanism. Exact measure is therefore a functional relation among material, volume, and user, not decorative precision.

#### Subchannel C. Circulating Service, Mixing, and Drinking
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Servers circulate with vessels, mix the contents, and bring the drink to a community of recipients.
- Active motifs: service by circling repeatedly (quranic:root_000957:B001/m01); circulating group or portion (quranic:root_000957:B004/m01); night watch or guarding circuit (quranic:root_000957:B005/m01); giving drink (quranic:root_000722:B001/m01); taking liquid (quranic:root_000783:B001/m01); community of drinkers (quranic:root_000783:B003/m01); drinking place and vessel (quranic:root_000783:B004/m01); cup with its contents (quranic:root_001277:B001/m01); mixing liquid (quranic:root_001417:B001/m01); honey used as a mixture (quranic:root_001417:B003/m01); drinking by a cup (quranic:root_001328:B005/m01); nurture carried through stages (quranic:root_000532:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:15, 76:19 (ط و ف); 76:17, 76:21 (س ق ي); 76:17 (ك ء س, م ز ج); 76:21 (ش ر ب, ر ب ب)
- Synthesis: The scene has a service loop: circulate, guard access, mix, pour, and deliver. The drinkers form a receiving community, while the Lord-root’s nurturing sense frames service as sustained care through stages. This is distinct from the vessel workshop: its invariant is movement and transfer, not fabrication.

### 13. P2 - The Arika as Settled Chamber and Living Grove
- Semantic invariant: The couch-root joins furnished rest to rooted residence among trees and pasture.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:13 names reclining on couches.
- Surprising reach: The same root that names the furnished couch also names the arak tree, animals that graze it, staying in place, and a named landscape, embedding furniture in an orchard ecology.

#### Subchannel A. Reclining in a Stable Interior
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person leans on a furnished support within a stable place prepared for residence and hospitality.
- Active motifs: couch in a curtained chamber (quranic:root_000026:B004/m01); staying in place (quranic:root_000026:B003/m01); leaning on a support (quranic:root_001678:B004/m01); fullness and retained contents (quranic:root_001678:B007/m01); leather cushion (quranic:root_000758:B006/m01); stability and settlement (quranic:root_001215:B003/m01); place and rank derived from being (quranic:root_001332:B002/m01); guest provision prepared for an arrival (quranic:root_001492:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:13 (ء ر ك, و ك ء); 76:15-16 (ق ر ر); 76:21 (س و ر); 76:22 (ك و ن); 76:23 (ن ز ل)
- Synthesis: Reclining is grounded in a complete interior scene: couch, support, cushion, stable place, retained fullness, and prepared provision. Residence is not temporary idleness; the place has enough structural and hospitable permanence to receive and keep its occupants.

#### Subchannel B. Couch-Root Reopens into Tree, Pasture, and Place
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: The furnished arika is lexically returned to an arak grove in which trees, grazing animals, fixed residence, and landscape converge.
- Active motifs: arak tree (quranic:root_000026:B001/m01); camels grazing and habituated to arak (quranic:root_000026:B002/m01); remaining in a place (quranic:root_000026:B003/m01); couch in a curtained chamber (quranic:root_000026:B004/m01); digestive effect of eating arak (quranic:root_000026:B006/m01); named mountain, valley, or place (quranic:root_000026:B007/m01); green tender vegetation (quranic:root_000418:B002/m01); near picked cluster (quranic:root_001241:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:13 (ء ر ك); 76:14 (ق ط ف); 76:21 (خ ض ر)
- Synthesis: The arika carries its own ecological setting. Tree, grazer, residence, and named place form a grove scene around the couch, while green vegetation and near fruit complete the environment. This does not reduce furniture to etymology; it shows how the resting place and orchard can occupy one coherent latent scene.

### 14. P2 - Circulating Attendants as Scattered Living Pearls
- Semantic invariant: Durable youthful attendants move in an ordered circuit and are perceived as a distributed field of brilliance.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:19 describes immortal youths circling and being taken for scattered pearls.
- Surprising reach: Permanence also denotes fixed jewelry, while circulation also denotes guarding; the attendants become both service network and living ornament.

#### Subchannel A. Durable Youth in a Service-and-Guard Circuit
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Young attendants persist in role, circle the gathering, and form a mobile service perimeter.
- Active motifs: offspring or child (quranic:root_001683:B001/m01); youthful boy or servant (quranic:root_001683:B004/m01); lasting state resistant to decay (quranic:root_000429:B001/m01); ornament fixed at ear or hand (quranic:root_000429:B003/m01); repeated circulation and service (quranic:root_000957:B001/m01); encircling group (quranic:root_000957:B004/m01); patrolling guard (quranic:root_000957:B005/m01); visible mutual facing (quranic:root_000531:B004/m01); leading member followed by the group (quranic:root_001444:B008/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:19 (و ل د, خ ل د, ط و ف, ر ء ي); 76:20 (م ل ك)
- Synthesis: Youth, durability, and circular movement form an organized service body. The guard-sense adds perimeter care, and the leader-sense gives the moving group order. Fixed jewelry provides the invariant with the next scene: these servants are durable ornaments, but ornaments that move and act.

#### Subchannel B. Vision Estimates a Field of Sparkle and Scatter
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An observer sees, estimates by resemblance, and resolves many moving figures into scattered points of pearl-like light.
- Active motifs: direct visual perception (quranic:root_000531:B001/m01); visible appearance or mirror-image (quranic:root_000531:B006/m01); making something visible to another (quranic:root_000531:B012/m01); counting and calculation (quranic:root_000318:B001/m01); estimation or supposition (quranic:root_000318:B002/m01); pearl (quranic:root_001337:B001/m01); visible sparkle (quranic:root_001337:B002/m01); flashing eye and hands (quranic:root_001337:B004/m01); casting many items apart (quranic:root_001472:B001/m01); detached shining fragment (quranic:root_001162:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:19 (ر ء ي, ح س ب, ل ء ل ء, ن ث ر); 76:15-16, 76:21 (ف ض ض); 76:20 (ر ء ي)
- Synthesis: The comparison is reconstructed as a perceptual process: seeing produces an estimate, estimation counts and distributes, and sparkle reduces moving bodies to bright points. Scatter is spatial rather than disorderly, because the preceding service circuit supplies the pattern within which the luminous points move.

### 15. P2 - Work Returns as Garment, Ornament, and Thanks
- Semantic invariant: Purposeful effort returns to its doer as acknowledged recompense and is then worn as clothing and encircling ornament.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:21-22 places garments and bracelets immediately before the declaration that the reward corresponds to a thanked striving.
- Surprising reach: The garment-root itself denotes return and recompense, turning apparel into the material form of an action coming back to its agent.

#### Subchannel A. Striving Enters a Recompense Ledger
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Directed work is completed, evaluated, returned to its agent, and publicly acknowledged as sufficient.
- Active motifs: directed movement toward a goal (quranic:root_000709:B001/m01); work, earning, and serious effort (quranic:root_000709:B002/m01); pursuit of generous public good (quranic:root_000709:B006/m01); corresponding recompense (quranic:root_000244:B001/m01); substitute that discharges a liability (quranic:root_000244:B002/m01); reward returning from one’s act (quranic:root_000209:B002/m01); recognition and praise for a favor (quranic:root_000810:B001/m01); small input made visibly sufficient (quranic:root_000810:B002/m01); occurrence made present (quranic:root_001332:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:21 (ث و ب); 76:22 (ج ز ي, س ع ي, ش ك ر, ك و ن)
- Synthesis: The scene is an exact return path. Effort moves toward a goal, becomes an assessable act, and comes back as recompense. Thanks does not remain a private feeling; it makes the work’s value manifest. Substitution adds the juridical effect that the returned reward fully answers what was due.

#### Subchannel B. Returning Value Takes the Form of Dress
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Recompense is converted into clothing whose material, breadth, color, and protective coverage embody the returned value.
- Active motifs: return to a prior place or state (quranic:root_000209:B001/m01); garment and clothing (quranic:root_000209:B003/m01); green color (quranic:root_000418:B001/m01); green tender vegetation (quranic:root_000418:B002/m01); flourishing ease and good life (quranic:root_000418:B003/m01); dye saturating cloth (quranic:root_000722:B009/m01); broad, ample garment or armor (quranic:root_001162:B005/m01); armor spread over the body (quranic:root_001472:B005/m01); strong leather garment (quranic:root_000666:B007/m01); fine fabric made from goat hair (quranic:root_000748:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:15-16, 76:21 (ف ض ض); 76:17, 76:21 (س ق ي); 76:19 (ن ث ر); 76:21 (ث و ب, خ ض ر); 76:26 (س ب ح); unavailable for س ن د س
- Synthesis: Returned action becomes a surface worn on the body. The garment is broad enough to cover, can shade into protective armor, and receives its green color as dye saturates material. The vegetation senses of green make the cloth carry flourishing life, while the return-sense of the garment-root keeps it tied to recompense rather than detached luxury.

#### Subchannel C. Bracelet as Encircling Rank
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Silver is formed into an ornament that encloses the wrist and simultaneously signifies elevated station.
- Active motifs: wearable ornament (quranic:root_000353:B001/m01); descriptive beauty or form (quranic:root_000353:B002/m01); bracelet encircling the hand (quranic:root_000758:B004/m01); elevated enclosed unit or rank (quranic:root_000758:B003/m01); high surrounding wall (quranic:root_000758:B002/m01); silver (quranic:root_001162:B003/m01); permanent earring or bracelet (quranic:root_000429:B003/m01); pearl jewel (quranic:root_001337:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:19 (خ ل د, ل ء ل ء); 76:21 (ح ل ي, س و ر, ف ض ض)
- Synthesis: The bracelet’s circular form is shared with wall and enclosed unit, while the same root carries elevated rank. Ornament therefore materializes station as a silver boundary worn on the body. Permanence and pearl extend the piece into the broader visual language of durable reward.

### 16. P2 - Bliss as a Cohesive and Water-Sustained Estate
- Semantic invariant: Good life is organized as a stable estate whose rule, central routes, hierarchy, and water supply hold its inhabitants together.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:20 reveals expansive bliss and a great dominion, with the surrounding ayahs furnishing residence, service, water, and dress.
- Surprising reach: Kingship includes the physical cohesion of kneaded material and the water by which a traveling group controls its affairs, grounding dominion in infrastructure rather than spectacle alone.

#### Subchannel A. Soft Living and Accepted Lodging
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Benefit, softness, secure shelter, and a place congenial to its occupants define an inhabitable good life.
- Active motifs: good condition and bestowed benefit (quranic:root_001525:B001/m01); softness and ease of living (quranic:root_001525:B002/m01); place agreeing with and pleasing its occupant (quranic:root_001525:B011/m01); repose and delight of the eye (quranic:root_001525:B013/m01); flourishing ease (quranic:root_000418:B003/m01); honored protective shelter (quranic:root_000966:B004/m01); guest provision (quranic:root_001492:B005/m01); need answered as blessing (quranic:root_000532:B016/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:14 (ظ ل ل); 76:20 (ن ع م); 76:21 (خ ض ر); 76:23 (ن ز ل); 76:24-25, 76:29 (ر ب ب)
- Synthesis: Bliss is specified as a successful habitation: the place agrees with its occupants, living is soft without fragility, the eye settles, shelter protects, and provision is already prepared. The need-and-blessing sense keeps comfort relational: the estate answers a need rather than merely displaying wealth.

#### Subchannel B. Dominion Held Together by Road, Water, and Leadership
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A realm is made cohesive, governed, centered on a route, supplied by water, and conducted by a leading figure.
- Active motifs: material cohesion and structural strength (quranic:root_001444:B001/m01); ownership and delegated control (quranic:root_001444:B002/m01); rule and sovereignty (quranic:root_001444:B003/m01); mainstay on which an affair depends (quranic:root_001444:B005/m01); central road or valley (quranic:root_001444:B006/m01); water as control of settlement (quranic:root_001444:B007/m01); leader at the head of a group (quranic:root_001444:B008/m01); great size or number (quranic:root_001281:B001/m01); governing share of an affair (quranic:root_001281:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:20 (م ل ك, ك ب ر)
- Synthesis: Dominion is reconstructed as an operating estate. Its material must hold, authority must be delegated, a central line must organize movement, water must sustain residence, and a leader must conduct the group. Greatness is therefore systemic scale—the size of an ordered whole—not only elevated status.

#### Subchannel C. Greatness as Rank, Weight, and Burden-Bearing Capacity
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Magnitude becomes recognized rank and the capacity to carry something weighty without losing cohesion.
- Active motifs: seeing something as great in the breast (quranic:root_001281:B003/m01); elevated rank and leadership (quranic:root_001281:B005/m01); greatness and majesty (quranic:root_001281:B006/m01); greatness that is difficult to carry (quranic:root_001281:B010/m01); high position and honor (quranic:root_001042:B002/m01); prevailing control (quranic:root_001042:B004/m01); upper level borne above a load (quranic:root_001042:B008/m01); firm structural cohesion (quranic:root_001444:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:20 (م ل ك, ك ب ر); 76:21 (ع ل و)
- Synthesis: “Great” is not exhausted by visual scale. It can be inwardly recognized, socially ranked, structurally borne, and difficult to carry. The elevation-root adds upper load and prevailing control, so the kingdom’s greatness includes the capacity to support a layered order.

### 17. P2 - Descent Gathered into Recitation
