- Semantic invariant: Revelation moves downward into an ordered place, where dispersed words are gathered, voiced, studied, and made a devotional practice.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:23 states the repeated sending down of the Quran, followed by commands that govern its reception and enactment.
- Surprising reach: The descent-root also names prepared guest provision, making revelation something delivered to and hosted by a receiving community.

#### Subchannel A. Lowering, Delivery, and Placement in a Proper Station
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A text is brought from above, delivered, and installed in the rank or place appropriate to it.
- Active motifs: descent from above (quranic:root_001492:B001/m01); delivery of book, water, clothing, or gift (quranic:root_001492:B002/m01); ordering something into its proper station (quranic:root_001492:B004/m01); prepared provision for a guest (quranic:root_001492:B005/m01); recollection restoring presence (quranic:root_000516:B003/m01); naming and public utterance (quranic:root_000516:B004/m01); reminder (quranic:root_000516:B009/m01); lordship and ownership (quranic:root_000532:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:23 (ن ز ل); 76:24-25, 76:29 (ر ب ب); 76:25, 76:29 (ذ ك ر)
- Synthesis: Descent is both motion and placement. The revelation is delivered, then ordered into a station from which it can be recalled and spoken. The guest-provision sense links delivery with reception: the text arrives as prepared sustenance that must be hosted, not merely as information that passes through.

#### Subchannel B. Gathering Words into Reading, Study, and Devotion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Linguistic units are collected, recited to others, studied together, and inhabited by a reader whose reading becomes worship.
- Active motifs: gathering and joining (quranic:root_001210:B001/m01); reading, reciting, and teaching another (quranic:root_001210:B002/m01); devotional reader and learned worshipper (quranic:root_001210:B006/m01); method, route, and patterned composition (quranic:root_001210:B011/m01); gathered wording and recitation (quranic:root_001211:B001/m01); poetic or textual pattern (quranic:root_001211:B005/m01); transmitting a greeting in words (quranic:root_001211:B006/m01); reminder restoring the absent object (quranic:root_000516:B009/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:23 (ق ر ء); 76:25, 76:29 (ذ ك ر)
- Synthesis: Quran is materialized as collected speech. Gathering produces a coherent wording; recitation transfers it; mutual study stabilizes it; pattern gives it a repeatable route; and the reader becomes a devotional role. Reminder then describes the outcome: what might become absent is repeatedly brought back into active presence.

### 18. P2 - The Bridle of Judgment and Competing Controls over Action
- Semantic invariant: Patience and divine judgment restrain action toward repair, while sinful command and induced obedience attempt to capture the same faculty of compliance.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:24 commands patience for the Lord’s judgment and forbids obedience to sinner or ingrate.
- Surprising reach: Judgment’s root includes the bridle that checks a horse, making the verse a contest over who controls the agent’s movement.

#### Subchannel A. Patience and Judgment as Corrective Restraint
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The self is held steady under a ruling that blocks corruption and guides movement as a bridle guides an animal.
- Active motifs: holding the self from panic (quranic:root_000840:B001/m01); coercive restraint distinguished from patience (quranic:root_000840:B002/m01); surety and staying with an obligation (quranic:root_000840:B003/m01); no-exit severity (quranic:root_000840:B006/m01); prevention of injustice for reform (quranic:root_000348:B001/m01); just adjudication (quranic:root_000348:B002/m01); precise, secure construction (quranic:root_000348:B004/m01); bridle restraining an animal’s jaws (quranic:root_000348:B006/m01); lordship and ownership (quranic:root_000532:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:24 (ص ب ر, ح ك م, ر ب ب); 76:30 (ح ك م)
- Synthesis: Patience is voluntary self-command, not mere immobility, and judgment is prevention ordered toward repair. The bridle sense makes the relation kinetic: a ruling checks uncontrolled motion and keeps the agent on course. Coercive patience remains present as a contrast, clarifying why the commanded hold must be aligned with the Lord rather than imposed by a corrupt human controller.

#### Subchannel B. Sinful Command Attempts to Capture Obedience
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A competing authority seeks compliance, slows movement toward good, and makes wrongdoing appear easy or compulsory.
- Active motifs: compliance and following an order (quranic:root_000956:B001/m01); agreement between two parties (quranic:root_000956:B002/m01); the self making an act easy (quranic:root_000956:B006/m01); delay from good (quranic:root_000013:B001/m01); recoiling from sin (quranic:root_000013:B002/m01); penalty corresponding to sin (quranic:root_000013:B003/m01); loading another with sin (quranic:root_000013:B004/m01); covering truth (quranic:root_001307:B003/m01); concealing a favor (quranic:root_001307:B004/m01); forcing the obedient into disobedience (quranic:root_001307:B007/m01); compulsion toward an affair (quranic:root_000831:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:24 (ط و ع, ء ث م, ك ف ر); 76:28-31 (ش ي ء)
- Synthesis: The prohibited obedience is a mechanism of captured agency. Sin delays the agent from good, a commander tries to load wrongdoing onto another, and forced disobedience converts compliance into complicity. The self-easing sense shows the inward counterpart: control succeeds when the commanded act is made to feel readily traversable.

### 19. P2 - A Day-Night Devotional Circuit
- Semantic invariant: Repeated remembrance and embodied worship occupy the full daily cycle from first light through evening into an extended night.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:25-26 commands remembrance in the morning and evening, then prostration and glorification through a long night.
- Surprising reach: Morning’s root supplies a pulley and glorification’s root supplies swimming or orbital motion, turning the schedule into a repeated mechanical and cosmic circuit.

#### Subchannel A. Morning and Evening Marked by Name and Recall
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The day is opened and closed by recalling, voicing, and elevating the divine name.
- Active motifs: inward recollection (quranic:root_000516:B003/m01); voiced mention (quranic:root_000516:B004/m01); reminder that renews presence (quranic:root_000516:B009/m01); name as designation (quranic:root_000745:B005/m01); good public elevation of a name (quranic:root_000745:B008/m01); first part of the day (quranic:root_000143:B001/m01); beginning and first yield (quranic:root_000143:B002/m01); late afternoon and evening (quranic:root_000038:B003/m01); morning and evening as paired times (quranic:root_001215:B009/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:15-16 (ق ر ر); 76:18, 76:25 (س م و); 76:25 (ذ ك ر, ب ك ر, ء ص ل)
- Synthesis: Naming is distributed across the day’s boundaries. Recollection makes the referent inwardly present, utterance gives it voice, and the name-root raises it into public notice. First light and evening create a repeated frame, so remembrance becomes a temporal practice rather than an isolated speech act.

#### Subchannel B. Long Night as Bodily Descent and Expansive Motion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The worshipper lowers the body, remains through extended darkness, and sends praise through a wide field of motion.
- Active motifs: night and its darkness (quranic:root_001392:B001/m01); activity undertaken at night (quranic:root_001392:B002/m01); adjacent nightly cycle (quranic:root_001392:B003/m01); prostration and humble submission (quranic:root_000675:B001/m01); place of prostration (quranic:root_000675:B002/m01); bodily points and marks of prostration (quranic:root_000675:B003/m01); bowing and lowering the head (quranic:root_000675:B004/m01); worship through glorification (quranic:root_000666:B001/m01); declaring transcendence and freedom from defect (quranic:root_000666:B002/m01); swimming, running, or orbital motion (quranic:root_000666:B004/m01); wide movement for one’s work (quranic:root_000666:B005/m01); temporal extension (quranic:root_000959:B003/m01); deliberate prolonging (quranic:root_000959:B007/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:26 (ل ي ل, س ج د, س ب ح, ط و ل)
- Synthesis: The night practice joins vertical descent to horizontal and orbital extension. The body lowers at specified points, while glorification ranges like swimming or celestial motion through a prolonged duration. The scene therefore fills the night both bodily and temporally.

#### Subchannel C. The Repeated Cycle as Pulley, Orbit, and Lunar Station
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Time is pictured as a mechanism that returns through stations while devotional action repeatedly draws and circulates.
- Active motifs: rotating well pulley (quranic:root_000143:B006/m01); taking the moon through its nightly stations (quranic:root_000018:B008/m01); swimming or orbital course (quranic:root_000666:B004/m01); entering and traversing night (quranic:root_001392:B002/m01); repeated time and recurrence (quranic:root_000989:B005/m01); time and cycle of an occurrence (quranic:root_001211:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:23 (ق ر ء); 76:25 (ب ك ر); 76:26 (س ب ح, ل ي ل); 76:29 (ء خ ذ); 76:31 (ع د د)
- Synthesis: Pulley, orbit, lunar station, and recurring count share a cycle signature. The mechanism repeatedly draws through fixed positions rather than advancing once and disappearing. That pattern gives concrete shape to worship distributed across morning, evening, and night.

### 20. P2 - Premature Taking versus Gestational Weight
- Semantic invariant: The immediately desired object is taken before maturation, whereas the deferred day gathers weight, value, and gestational consequence.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:27 contrasts love of the immediate with leaving behind a heavy day.
- Surprising reach: Haste includes premature birth and quick rations, while heaviness includes pregnancy, making the temporal contrast one between premature consumption and a consequence still developing toward delivery.

#### Subchannel A. The Immediate as Speed, Shortcut, Quick Ration, and Prematurity
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Desire accelerates acquisition, shortens the road, prepares portable provision, and takes an outcome before its full term.
- Active motifs: enduring attachment or preference (quranic:root_000286:B002/m01); haste and bringing forward (quranic:root_000987:B001/m01); light portable waterskin (quranic:root_000987:B004/m01); hastily prepared food or ration (quranic:root_000987:B005/m01); premature delivery that survives (quranic:root_000987:B007/m01); quick road or shortcut (quranic:root_000987:B008/m01); the near or lower world (quranic:root_000493:B002/m01); patience and delay (quranic:root_000063:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:14 (د ن و); 76:15 (ء ن ي); 76:27 (ح ب ب, ع ج ل)
- Synthesis: The immediate is a compact logistics scene: choose the short road, carry a small skin, eat the ration prepared in haste, and force delivery before full term. Delay stands as the excluded operation. This makes attachment to the present a preference for availability over maturation.

#### Subchannel B. The Deferred Day as Weight, Value, Slowness, and Pregnancy
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A future event accumulates mass and worth, slows movement, and develops like a carried pregnancy.
- Active motifs: physical and figurative weight (quranic:root_000202:B001/m01); precious object of weight and rank (quranic:root_000202:B005/m01); heaviness, sleepiness, and slow movement (quranic:root_000202:B006/m01); pregnancy as a carried burden (quranic:root_000202:B007/m01); impaired reception by the ear (quranic:root_000202:B009/m01); calamitous event embodied as a day (quranic:root_001700:B003/m01); greatness difficult to carry (quranic:root_001281:B010/m01); extended duration (quranic:root_000959:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:20 (ك ب ر); 76:26 (ط و ل); 76:27 (ث ق ل, ي و م)
- Synthesis: The heavy day is not merely unpleasant. It is valuable enough to have weight, slow enough to resist hurried movement, and pregnant with an outcome that has not yet been delivered. Dull hearing adds an epistemic effect: attachment to the immediate makes the coming weight difficult to receive.

#### Subchannel C. Leaving Behind as Cutting, Veiling, and Banked Fire
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The future is abandoned by cutting it off from attention and placing it behind a screen, though energy remains banked there.
- Active motifs: leaving or abandoning (quranic:root_001638:B003/m01); cutting flesh or opening a wound (quranic:root_001638:B002/m01); hiding and substituting an apparent meaning (quranic:root_001642:B005/m01); the side behind, beyond, or across a barrier (quranic:root_001642:B006/m01); fire latent in a fire-stick (quranic:root_001642:B002/m01); humanity on the earth (quranic:root_001642:B008/m01); covering and concealment (quranic:root_001307:B001/m01); descending calamity (quranic:root_001492:B006/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:23 (ن ز ل); 76:24 (ك ف ر); 76:27 (و ذ ر, و ر ي)
- Synthesis: Abandonment is more forceful than forgetting. The object is cut away and put behind a veil, but the fire-stick sense says that what is hidden can remain charged and ready to ignite. The future day is therefore concealed from present regard, not deprived of causal force.

### 21. P2 - Body Binding and Substitutive Redesign
- Semantic invariant: A body is measured, created, articulated, and strengthened; the same design authority can replace it with another likeness or altered form.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:28 joins creation, strengthened bodily binding, will, substitution, and likeness.
- Surprising reach: Replacement is modeled as both exchanging one unit for another and changing an existing image, so divine redesign covers identity, form, and manufacture without collapsing them.

#### Subchannel A. Measured Creation, Jointing, and Structural Strength
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A maker determines dimensions, creates a balanced form, binds its members, and brings it to structural maturity.
- Active motifs: measuring before fabrication (quranic:root_000434:B001/m01); creating and bringing into being (quranic:root_000434:B002/m01); balanced bodily form (quranic:root_000434:B003/m01); firm joints and articulated constitution (quranic:root_000030:B002/m01); entirety with all its binding (quranic:root_000030:B005/m01); tightening a knot or bond (quranic:root_000782:B001/m01); strength and solidity (quranic:root_000782:B002/m01); maturity of power and judgment (quranic:root_000782:B004/m01); structural cohesion (quranic:root_001444:B001/m01); interlocking members and connective tissue (quranic:root_000464:B008/m01); bodily temperament and composition (quranic:root_001417:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:17 (م ز ج); 76:20 (م ل ك); 76:28 (خ ل ق, ش د د, ء س ر); 76:31 (د خ ل)
- Synthesis: The body is treated as a designed assembly. Measurement precedes creation; balanced form receives joints; knots and connective tissue hold the whole; and maturity describes the completed strength of the system. “Binding” in 76:28 is thus anatomically literalized without losing its force as control.

#### Subchannel B. Exchange, Image Change, and Reproducible Pattern
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: One created unit can be exchanged for another, its form altered, or a new instance produced from an intelligible model.
- Active motifs: one thing standing in place of another (quranic:root_000095:B001/m01); changing form while substance may remain (quranic:root_000095:B002/m01); general likeness and equivalence (quranic:root_001397:B001/m01); standing present as a form (quranic:root_001397:B005/m01); made image or model (quranic:root_001397:B008/m01); following a model or pattern (quranic:root_001397:B010/m01); recovery toward a comparable healthy state (quranic:root_001397:B012/m01); creative production (quranic:root_000434:B002/m01); balanced visible form (quranic:root_000434:B003/m01); will directed to bringing about a thing (quranic:root_000831:B002/m01); newly generated product (quranic:root_001683:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:19 (و ل د); 76:28 (ب د ل, خ ل ق, م ث ل, ش ي ء)
- Synthesis: Substitution has three distinct operations: exchange the occupant of a place, alter the visible form, or reproduce according to a model. Presence and recovery show that likeness can govern both replacement and restoration. Will is the initiating agency that selects among these design operations.

### 22. P2 - Reminder Becomes a Taken Route under Nested Will
- Semantic invariant: A reminder supplies signs and a route; taking that route is a real human act whose possibility remains enclosed by divine will, knowledge, and wisdom.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:29-30 joins reminder, willing, taking a path to the Lord, divine willing, knowledge, and wisdom.
- Surprising reach: “Taking a path” activates senses of grasping, acquiring, and adopting a people’s pattern, making volition a sustained appropriation rather than a momentary preference.

#### Subchannel A. Reminder, Marker, Grasp, and Route
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A reminder re-presents the destination, a visible marker identifies the road, and the traveler grasps and adopts it as a way of life.
- Active motifs: inward recollection (quranic:root_000516:B003/m01); documentary record of a right (quranic:root_000516:B008/m01); reminder restoring presence (quranic:root_000516:B009/m01); marker, banner, or road sign (quranic:root_001040:B002/m01); grasping and taking possession (quranic:root_000018:B001/m01); adopting a people’s way and form (quranic:root_000018:B009/m01); acquiring something for oneself (quranic:root_000018:B010/m01); traversable road (quranic:root_000672:B001/m01); traveler on the road (quranic:root_000672:B002/m01); goal and terminal limit (quranic:root_000076:B001/m01); lordship and destination-owner (quranic:root_000532:B001/m01); patterned way or method (quranic:root_001210:B011/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:23 (ق ر ء); 76:29 (ذ ك ر, ء خ ذ, س ب ل, ر ب ب); 76:30 (ع ل م); unavailable for ء ل ي
- Synthesis: The reminder does not merely announce a road. It reactivates a claim, provides a marker, and presents a path that must be grasped, acquired, and adopted as a pattern. The destination is relational—the path is “to the Lord”—so taking it also means entering the order of its owner.

#### Subchannel B. Human Will Nested within Divine Will
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Human willing initiates a choice, but the act’s existence, capacity, and completion remain conditioned by a more encompassing will.
- Active motifs: will as creating or attaining a thing (quranic:root_000831:B002/m01); will and volition (quranic:root_000832:B001/m01); acquisition for oneself (quranic:root_000018:B010/m01); capability and effective power (quranic:root_001205:B003/m01); capacity to perform an act (quranic:root_000956:B003/m01); exerted ability and effort (quranic:root_000076:B011/m01); occurrence in time (quranic:root_001332:B001/m01); divine name (quranic:root_000047:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:16 (ق د ر); 76:22, 76:30 (ك و ن); 76:24 (ط و ع); 76:29-31 (ش ي ء); 76:29 (ء خ ذ); 76:30 (ء ل ه); unavailable for ء ل ي
- Synthesis: The two will motifs preserve both agency and dependence. Human willing is an act of acquisition; ability makes it executable; occurrence gives it temporal reality. Yet all three remain inside divine willing. Exerted capacity clarifies that the nesting concerns effective power, not verbal intention alone.

#### Subchannel C. Knowledge and Wisdom Govern the Route
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Comprehensive knowledge identifies reality and its signs, while wisdom fixes the proper measure and prevents corrupt movement.
- Active motifs: knowledge as disclosure to the knower (quranic:root_001040:B001/m01); distinguishing sign and guide (quranic:root_001040:B002/m01); accurate wisdom and understanding (quranic:root_000348:B003/m01); secure and exact ordering (quranic:root_000348:B004/m01); learned orientation to the Lord (quranic:root_000532:B003/m01); effective capacity (quranic:root_001205:B003/m01); measured planning (quranic:root_001205:B005/m01); prevention and reform (quranic:root_000348:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:16 (ق د ر); 76:24, 76:30 (ح ك م); 76:24-25, 76:29 (ر ب ب); 76:30 (ع ل م)
- Synthesis: Knowledge discloses both object and marker; wisdom converts that disclosure into sound ordering and corrective restraint. Capacity and planning ensure that governance is operative rather than contemplative. The path of 76:29 is therefore held inside an epistemic and practical administration.

### 23. P2 - Mercy’s Interior versus Prepared Pain
- Semantic invariant: Mercy admits and encloses within a nurturing interior, while injustice misplaces, withholds, and prepares an encounter whose defining outcome is pain.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:31 contrasts admission into mercy with prepared painful punishment for wrongdoers.
- Surprising reach: The torment-root begins from sweetness, abstention, and withholding, so punishment appears as the violent inversion or deprivation of the drink that saturates the preceding paradise.

#### Subchannel A. Entering a Nurturing Interior
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A recipient is admitted into an inside characterized by care, kinship, generative enclosure, and sustained guardianship.
- Active motifs: entering an interior (quranic:root_000464:B001/m01); inward secret or lining (quranic:root_000464:B003/m01); interlocking interior parts (quranic:root_000464:B008/m01); mercy as tenderness and beneficence (quranic:root_000552:B001/m01); kinship bond (quranic:root_000552:B002/m01); womb as generative enclosure (quranic:root_000552:B003/m01); nurture through stages (quranic:root_000532:B002/m01); guardianship and standing surety (quranic:root_001332:B003/m01); prepared hospitality (quranic:root_001492:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:22, 76:30 (ك و ن); 76:23 (ن ز ل); 76:24-25, 76:29 (ر ب ب); 76:31 (د خ ل, ر ح م)
- Synthesis: Admission is a movement across a boundary into care. The interior is lined, articulated, and held together; mercy supplies tenderness, kinship, and the womb’s generative enclosure; nurture and guardianship sustain what has entered. The result is not abstract pardon but secure incorporation.

#### Subchannel B. Wrong Placement, Withheld Right, and Prepared Pain
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Wrongdoing puts acts outside their proper place, withholds what is due, and prepares an apparatus whose action produces suffering.
- Active motifs: grievance and demand for redress (quranic:root_000967:B003/m01); putting something in the wrong time or place (quranic:root_000967:B004/m01); withholding and confining a right (quranic:root_000967:B008/m01); preparing equipment and reserve (quranic:root_000989:B002/m01); punishment and severe infliction (quranic:root_000994:B005/m01); felt pain (quranic:root_000046:B001/m01); causing pain or being painful (quranic:root_000046:B002/m01); penalty corresponding to sin (quranic:root_000013:B003/m01); inward concealed corruption (quranic:root_000464:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:24 (ء ث م); 76:31 (د خ ل, ظ ل م, ع د د, ع ذ ب, ء ل م)
- Synthesis: Wrongdoing is defined spatially and relationally before it is penal: it dislocates an act and confines another’s right. Preparation then gives the consequence material readiness, and pain names both experienced state and inflicted operation. Concealed corruption supplies the inward counterpart to outward misplacement.

#### Subchannel C. Sweetness Withheld and Reversed into Torment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Sweet drink is first available and purifying; refusal, deprivation, or forced abstention turns the same lexical field toward punishment.
- Active motifs: sweet, palatable water or food (quranic:root_000994:B001/m01); body abstaining from drink under thirst (quranic:root_000994:B002/m01); withholding and weaning from an object (quranic:root_000994:B003/m01); severe punishment (quranic:root_000994:B005/m01); unpleasant liquid repeatedly forced on the heart (quranic:root_000722:B010/m01); bitter or brackish water drunk reluctantly (quranic:root_000783:B005/m01); water that purifies another (quranic:root_000953:B004/m01); flowing spring (quranic:root_001069:B006/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:17, 76:21 (س ق ي); 76:18 (ع ي ن); 76:21 (ش ر ب, ط ه ر); 76:31 (ع ذ ب)
- Synthesis: Sweetness and torment occupy one root but opposite operations. Paradise supplies drink; punishment withholds, forces abstention, or administers what the body rejects. Purifying water marks the positive pole, while an unwanted liquid repeatedly imposed on the heart materializes the negative one.

### 24. P2 - Gestational Renewal and Enduring Offspring
- Semantic invariant: Retained fluid develops toward birth, and the resulting offspring persist across generation and service.
- Surface relation: indirect; 76:19 supplies youths and permanence, 76:27 supplies gestational heaviness, 76:28 supplies re-creation and replacement, and 76:31 supplies the womb-root through mercy.
- Surprising reach: Reclining, glass stability, reading, and scattering carry labor, settled seed, womb-gathering, and prolific birth, making renewal a distributed reproductive scene.

#### Subchannel A. Retention, Labor, and Near Delivery
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Fluid is enclosed, retained, made to settle, carried as weight, and brought through labor toward birth.
- Active motifs: delivery drawing near (quranic:root_000493:B004/m01); laboring cry and movement of a she-camel (quranic:root_001678:B006/m01); bodily fullness and retention (quranic:root_001678:B007/m01); reproductive fluid settling and pregnancy becoming fixed (quranic:root_001215:B013/m01); pregnancy as heaviness (quranic:root_000202:B007/m01); womb enclosing a fetus (quranic:root_000552:B003/m01); womb pain after birth (quranic:root_000552:B004/m01); womb gathering pregnancy (quranic:root_001210:B004/m01); womb gathering pregnancy (quranic:root_001211:B003/m01); amniotic or abdominal fluid (quranic:root_000722:B005/m01); afterbirth and womb (quranic:root_000994:B009/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:13 (و ك ء); 76:14 (د ن و); 76:15-16 (ق ر ر); 76:17, 76:21 (س ق ي); 76:23 (ق ر ء); 76:27 (ث ق ل); 76:31 (ر ح م, ع ذ ب)
- Synthesis: The reproductive scene proceeds through exact states: enclosure, fullness, settling, increasing weight, nearness, labor, and postpartum consequence. The two womb-gathering motifs reinforce the same retaining operation at the center of gestation.

#### Subchannel B. Birth, Youth, Generation, and Persistence
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Birth produces young attendants, extends into later generations, and is stabilized as enduring life.
- Active motifs: offspring (quranic:root_001683:B001/m01); parents as sources of birth (quranic:root_001683:B002/m01); act of giving birth (quranic:root_001683:B003/m01); young child or servant (quranic:root_001683:B004/m01); generated product or new linguistic form (quranic:root_001683:B005/m01); age-peer (quranic:root_001683:B006/m01); prolific birth and offspring scattering (quranic:root_001472:B006/m01); lasting state (quranic:root_000429:B001/m01); grandchild coming behind the child (quranic:root_001642:B007/m01); foster child and caretaker (quranic:root_000532:B005/m01); first child and first birth (quranic:root_000143:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:19 (و ل د, خ ل د, ن ث ر); 76:24-25, 76:29 (ر ب ب); 76:25 (ب ك ر); 76:27 (و ر ي)
- Synthesis: The youths belong to a full generational frame: parents, birth, child, peer, foster relation, later descendant, and prolific multiplication. Persistence changes the scene from one delivery into maintained offspring. The generated-product sense also links biological renewal to the replacement mechanics of 76:28.

### 25. P2 - A Drink that Cleanses Medium and Recipient
- Semantic invariant: Pure liquid is both clean in itself and capable of transferring cleanliness into body, heart, and conduct.
- Surface relation: direct; 76:21 culminates the hospitality scene in a pure drink given by the Lord.
- Surprising reach: Drinking can mean color or love penetrating a substrate and heart, so purification is pictured as inward saturation rather than external washing alone.

#### Subchannel A. Clean Water as an Active Cleansing Medium
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Water is intrinsically clean, used for washing, and able to remove impurity from another object.
- Active motifs: cleanliness and removal of stain (quranic:root_000953:B001/m01); washing and ritual purification with water (quranic:root_000953:B003/m01); pure medium that purifies another (quranic:root_000953:B004/m01); giving drink (quranic:root_000722:B001/m01); flowing liquid (quranic:root_001162:B006/m01); spring source (quranic:root_001069:B006/m01); sweet potable water (quranic:root_000994:B001/m01); nurture and completion (quranic:root_000532:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:15-16, 76:21 (ف ض ض); 76:17, 76:21 (س ق ي); 76:18 (ع ي ن); 76:21 (ر ب ب, ط ه ر); 76:31 (ع ذ ب)
- Synthesis: Purity is an active property of the drink. The liquid originates in a spring, flows, is served, and can remove stain from another. Nurture makes cleansing developmental: what is washed is also brought toward completion.

#### Subchannel B. Purity Saturates Heart, Color, and Action
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Liquid penetrates a recipient as dye enters cloth or affection enters the heart, carrying moral cleansing inward.
- Active motifs: liquid, color, or love absorbed into a substrate and heart (quranic:root_000783:B007/m01); taking liquid into the body (quranic:root_000783:B001/m01); dye saturating cloth (quranic:root_000722:B009/m01); cleansing the self and action from ugliness (quranic:root_000953:B005/m01); clean garment as sound character (quranic:root_000953:B005/m02); bodily temperament formed by mixture (quranic:root_001417:B002/m01); recognition of favor (quranic:root_000810:B001/m01); worship through purification and transcendence (quranic:root_000666:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:17, 76:21 (س ق ي, م ز ج); 76:21 (ش ر ب, ط ه ر); 76:22 (ش ك ر); 76:26 (س ب ح)
- Synthesis: Purity moves inward by saturation. Drink enters the body; dye enters cloth; affection enters the heart; and moral cleansing enters action. The garment sense makes the result externally legible, while gratitude and glorification show the cleansed recipient responding in conduct.

### 26. P2 - Illumination without a Dominant Sun
- Semantic invariant: Light is distributed through stars, glass, silver, pearl, eyes, and green surfaces rather than concentrated in a single solar source.
- Surface relation: indirect; 76:13 removes the sun, while 76:15-21 repeatedly presents glass, silver, pearl-like attendants, seeing, and green dress.
- Surprising reach: Severe cold supplies stellar shimmer, and silver supplies flowing liquid and fragments, producing a cool, mobile luminosity rather than hot centralized glare.

#### Subchannel A. Cool Brilliance through Glass, Silver, Pearl, and Eye
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Light is refracted, fragmented, and reflected by cool materials and moving observers.
- Active motifs: stellar shimmer from the cold-root (quranic:root_000645:B003/m01); glass vessel (quranic:root_001215:B012/m01); silver (quranic:root_001162:B003/m01); detached shining fragments (quranic:root_001162:B004/m01); flowing silver-like liquid (quranic:root_001162:B006/m01); pearl (quranic:root_001337:B001/m01); sparkle like pearl or flame (quranic:root_001337:B002/m01); flashing eye and hands (quranic:root_001337:B004/m01); visible appearance and mirror (quranic:root_000531:B006/m01); eye as organ of sight (quranic:root_001069:B001/m01); sun’s disk and light (quranic:root_000818:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:13 (ش م س, ز م ه ر); 76:15-16 (ق ر ر, ف ض ض); 76:18 (ع ي ن); 76:19 (ر ء ي, ل ء ل ء); 76:20 (ر ء ي); 76:21 (ف ض ض)
- Synthesis: With direct sun removed, luminosity becomes distributed and cool. Stars shimmer, glass refracts, silver fragments and flows, pearls sparkle, and eyes and hands return flashes. The scene produces visibility through many moving reflectors rather than one overpowering source.

#### Subchannel B. Green as Living Color, Watered Surface, and Prosperity
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Green spreads from plant life and water into cloth, complexion, and the visual register of ease.
- Active motifs: green and near-black depth of color (quranic:root_000418:B001/m01); tender green vegetation (quranic:root_000418:B002/m01); flourishing prosperity (quranic:root_000418:B003/m01); watered milk taking a green cast (quranic:root_000418:B008/m01); green palm and fruit (quranic:root_000418:B011/m01); vessel greened by retained water (quranic:root_000418:B012/m01); grain changing between green and yellow (quranic:root_001417:B004/m01); dye saturating cloth (quranic:root_000722:B009/m01); garment (quranic:root_000209:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:17 (م ز ج, س ق ي); 76:21 (خ ض ر, ث و ب, س ق ي)
- Synthesis: Green is not a flat garment color. It is the visible effect of vegetation, retained water, ripening, and prosperity, then transferred into cloth by saturation. Its range into deep darkness prevents the scene from becoming monochrome brightness; life and water give the color depth.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. P1 - A Kitchen and Serving Workshop
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Raw grain, thick extract, honey, water, and flour are prepared with pots, cloths, filters, jars, cups, skins, and stoppers for serving.
- Active motifs: cloth for lowering a cooking pot (quranic:root_000248:B007/m01); flour cooked with milk (quranic:root_000306:B012/m01); large storage jar (quranic:root_000286:B007/m01); filter cloth over a pouring vessel (quranic:root_001102:B008/m01); bottle or well stopper (quranic:root_000840:B018/m01); prepared cup (quranic:root_000978:B004/m01); thick syrup or extract used to improve food (quranic:root_000532:B006/m01); conditioning and sealing a waterskin (quranic:root_000783:B013/m01); honey used as mixture (quranic:root_001417:B003/m01); tasting and eating food (quranic:root_000934:B001/m01); wheat and grain (quranic:root_000104:B006/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2 (ج ع ل); 76:4 (ع ت د, غ ل ل); 76:5 (ب ر ر, ش ر ب, م ز ج); 76:8-9 (ح ب ب, ط ع م); 76:10 (ر ب ب); 76:12 (ص ب ر, ح ر ر)
- Synthesis: The motifs complete a compact food-preparation scene: grain and flour become cooked food; honey and thick extract modify it; water is kept in a conditioned skin; jars and cups store or serve; cloths protect hands and filter liquid; a stopper controls closure. This materializes the pericope’s feeding and drinking through the labor and apparatus that make provision usable.

### S2. P1 - Trial as Wear, Service, Oath, and Disclosed Quality
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Repeated use wears material, testing reveals its condition, and good service, excuse, or oath makes quality publicly demonstrable.
- Active motifs: wear and fraying through use (quranic:root_000153:B001/m01); trial revealing quality (quranic:root_000153:B002/m01); benefaction and good service proven in action (quranic:root_000153:B003/m01); excuse or oath displayed to remove blame (quranic:root_000153:B004/m01); wear and decay through use (quranic:root_000154:B001/m01); trial disclosing condition (quranic:root_000154:B002/m01); excellent performance in service or conflict (quranic:root_000154:B003/m01); excuse made manifest (quranic:root_000154:B004/m01); oath offered for reassurance or testing (quranic:root_000154:B005/m01); garment worn smooth and threadbare (quranic:root_000434:B009/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2 (ب ل و, خ ل ق); unavailable for ب ل ي
- Synthesis: Testing is a material and evidentiary process. Use abrades a surface; trial exposes hidden quality; good performance demonstrates worth; and oath or excuse brings a contested state into the open. The parallel test motifs jointly complete one disclosure scene through their oath and service details.

### S3. Whole-surah - Camphor Prepared as Perfume and Incense
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Aromatic material is ground, compounded, applied, and burned to scent bodies, objects, and a gathering.
- Active motifs: camphor as perfume (quranic:root_001307:B011/m01); perfume-grinding slab (quranic:root_000973:B012/m01); compounded perfume applied as a coating (quranic:root_000434:B010/m01); aloe wood burned as incense (quranic:root_000076:B014/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2, 76:28 (خ ل ق); 76:3-5, 76:24 (ك ف ر); 76:6 (ع ب د); unavailable for ء ل ي
- Synthesis: Camphor is joined to a small perfumery practice: scent is prepared on a dedicated slab, compounded into an anointing substance, applied to a surface, and extended into the air by incense wood. This materializes the surah's camphor drink and clothed reward as multisensory hospitality whose fragrance must itself be prepared and diffused.

### S4. Whole-surah - A Burial with a Tethered Grave Animal
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A life is taken, the body is shrouded and placed within a grave, and a camel is tethered beside it without food or water until it dies.
- Active motifs: death as taking the soul (quranic:root_001669:B002/m01); concealing the dead in shroud and grave (quranic:root_000266:B009/m01); sides of the grave (quranic:root_000840:B004/m01); grave camel tethered until death (quranic:root_000153:B005/m01); grave camel denied fodder and water until death (quranic:root_000154:B006/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2 (ب ل و); 76:7 (و ف ي); 76:12 (ج ن ن, ص ب ر); 76:24 (ص ب ر); unavailable for ب ل ي
- Synthesis: The motifs form a specific funerary scene rather than a generic mortality field: soul-taking precedes concealment, shroud, grave enclosure, and the tethering of a living camel at the burial place. This usefully pressures the surah's primary ethics of feeding, watering, and releasing captives: the righteous sustain a hungry captive, whereas this grave practice immobilizes an animal and withholds exactly the food and drink the surah makes signs of mercy.

### S5. Whole-surah - A Contracted Slave Works toward Manumission
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A saleable captive is subjected to labor, earns the remaining price of the neck, and passes from bondage into legal freedom.
- Active motifs: owned and saleable slave (quranic:root_000973:B001/m01); enslavement and forced labor (quranic:root_000973:B004/m01); binding and captivity (quranic:root_000030:B001/m01); contracted slave earning the price of the neck (quranic:root_000709:B005/m01); freedom and release from servitude or confinement (quranic:root_000306:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:6 (ع ب د); 76:8, 76:28 (ء س ر); 76:12 (ح ر ر); 76:22 (س ع ي)
- Synthesis: Ownership, coercive labor, a remaining purchase price, the slave's earnings, and release form a complete manumission process. This reframes the surah's captive, servants, striving, and reward through the concrete economics of recovering one's own neck: feeding the captive supports life before freedom, while accepted striving culminates lexically in release rather than continued ownership.

### S6. Whole-surah - Marriage from Contract to Changed Status
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A marriage is contracted, a bride is conducted to her spouse, and the wedding night either fails to reach or completes consummation, changing personal status.
- Active motifs: marriage contract (quranic:root_001444:B004/m01); bride delivered to her husband (quranic:root_001583:B006/m01); virginity and absence of prior contact (quranic:root_000143:B004/m01); wedding night without consummation (quranic:root_000306:B016/m01); marital consummation (quranic:root_000464:B002/m01); post-consummation status (quranic:root_000209:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:3 (ه د ي); 76:12 (ح ر ر); 76:20 (م ل ك); 76:21 (ث و ب); 76:25 (ب ك ر); 76:31 (د خ ل)
- Synthesis: The cluster traces a socially ordered transition with a genuine branching outcome: contract and bridal conveyance establish the union, while the wedding night may leave virgin status intact or change it through consummation. This usefully pressures a purely biological reading of the surah's mixed drop and renewed creation by placing generation within covenant, bodily vulnerability, and a socially recognized change of status.

### S7. Whole-surah - A Raft Stabilized and Piloted across Water
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Inflated skins or tied wood form a raft, pitch seals a vessel, a stern or rudder checks its oscillation, and a chief sailor directs the crossing.
- Active motifs: raft of inflated skins or lashed wood (quranic:root_000957:B006/m01); pitched or tarred ship (quranic:root_000973:B005/m01); stern or rudder stabilizing a ship (quranic:root_000726:B008/m01); chief of the sailors (quranic:root_000532:B017/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:6 (ع ب د); 76:8 (س ك ن); 76:10, 76:21, 76:24-25, 76:29 (ر ب ب); 76:15, 76:19 (ط و ف)
- Synthesis: Buoyancy alone does not complete the crossing. The hull must be assembled or sealed, its lateral disturbance corrected by the stern, and its course entrusted to a pilot. This materializes the surah's guided path and nested will as governed navigation: the human craft floats and responds, but direction and stability depend on a controlling office evoked through the same root that names lordship.

### S8. Whole-surah - Purification, Call, and Bodily Prayer
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Purifying water is used in ablution, including nasal rinsing, before a repeated call gathers worshippers to a prayer place for prostration and counted glorification.
- Active motifs: water or implement that purifies another (quranic:root_000953:B004/m01); washing and ablution with water (quranic:root_000953:B003/m01); nasal clearing during purification (quranic:root_001472:B002/m01); repeated prayer call (quranic:root_000209:B004/m01); prayer place or mat (quranic:root_000675:B002/m01); bodily points and marks of prostration (quranic:root_000675:B003/m01); beads used to count glorification (quranic:root_000666:B006/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:19 (ن ث ر); 76:21 (ث و ب, ط ه ر); 76:26 (س ب ح, س ج د)
- Synthesis: The cluster supplies a full embodied rite: a cleansing medium acts on the worshipper, water is expelled from the nose, a repeated call marks assembly, a designated surface receives the body, and beads support repeated glorification. This materializes the commands to prostrate and glorify through water, place, limbs, sound, and counting, while reframing the pure drink as adjacent to a medium that prepares the body for worship.

### S9. Whole-surah - A Credit Sale Reconciled in Cash
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A seller sets a food price, a buyer transacts on credit to obtain cash, money or oil is weighed and counted, and the account is corrected to balance.
- Active motifs: market pricing, especially of food (quranic:root_000708:B003/m01); cash or present money opposed to debt (quranic:root_001069:B011/m01); purchase on credit or cash-seeking sale (quranic:root_001069:B012/m01); known unit for weighing money or oil (quranic:root_001677:B004/m01); counting an enumerable amount (quranic:root_000989:B001/m01); setting an account straight (quranic:root_000306:B005/m02)
- Ayah anchors: 76:4 (س ع ر); 76:6, 76:18 (ع ي ن); 76:11 (و ق ي); 76:12 (ح ر ر); 76:31 (ع د د)
- Synthesis: Price, credit, ready cash, standard weight, enumeration, and corrected account form one transaction rather than a finance catalog. This materializes the exchange ledger that 76:9 refuses: ordinary provision creates a priced and reconcilable obligation, so feeding without recompense or thanks becomes a sharper rejection of the market relation.

### S10. Whole-surah - A Text Corrected, Ordered, and Archived
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Words are received and taught, read aloud, corrected for malformed letters or omissions, placed in proper order, and stored in a book chest.
- Active motifs: receiving and teaching words or Quran (quranic:root_001372:B011/m01); reading, reciting, and teaching a text (quranic:root_001210:B002/m01); correcting letters and omitted text (quranic:root_000306:B005/m01); arranging material in its proper station (quranic:root_001492:B004/m01); reed chest used to preserve books (quranic:root_001257:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:10 (ق م ط ر); 76:11 (ل ق ي); 76:12 (ح ر ر); 76:23 (ق ر ء, ن ز ل)
- Synthesis: Reception does not end with utterance. The text moves through teaching and reading into correction, ordered placement, and protected storage, with the book chest completing the preservation scene. This materializes the surah's descent, recitation, and reminder as practices that resist textual loss and keep delivered words available for renewed reading.

### S11. Whole-surah - Voice, Drum, and Wind Instrument in Performance
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A listener receives cultivated song accompanied by a one-faced drum and an instrument set that can include drum, lute, or pipes sounded through a skin.
- Active motifs: hearing and attentive audition (quranic:root_000741:B001/m01); pleasurable singing or cultivated voice (quranic:root_000741:B007/m01); one-faced drum (quranic:root_001281:B012/m01); drum, lute, or grouped pipes played through a skin (quranic:root_001328:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2 (س م ع); 76:15 (ك و ب); 76:20 (ك ب ر)
- Synthesis: Singer, instruments, and listener complete a performance rather than a generic sound field. This usefully pressures the surah's primary movement from created hearing to Quranic recitation and repeated remembrance: audition is a receptive capacity that can be filled by pleasurable performance as well as by the demanding speech of guidance.

### S12. Whole-surah - A Gambling-Arrow Kit
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Smoothed lots, including the named al-mu'alla, are grouped and kept in a leather case as a maysir apparatus.
- Active motifs: smoothed arrow or lot (quranic:root_000434:B008/m02); leather container holding gambling arrows (quranic:root_000532:B010/m01); al-mu'alla among the gaming arrows (quranic:root_001042:B009/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2, 76:28 (خ ل ق); 76:10, 76:21, 76:24-25, 76:29 (ر ب ب); 76:21 (ع ل و)
- Synthesis: A polished lot, a named member of the set, and the case that keeps the gambling arrows together form a complete gaming apparatus. This usefully pressures the surah's final account of route and will: drawing a lot externalizes choice into chance, whereas 76:29-31 presents taking the route as willing action nested within divine will and knowledge.

### S13. Whole-surah - Dream, Apparition, Evil Eye, and Restraining Amulet
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A troubling dream or apparition is interpreted through jinn-mediated divination, an evil eye is diagnosed, and an amulet or spell is used to bind its effect or another person's agency.
- Active motifs: dream vision (quranic:root_000531:B003/m01); jinn appearing to disclose divination or a cure (quranic:root_000531:B008/m01); hidden jinn community (quranic:root_000266:B005/m01); passing apparition or visitation (quranic:root_000957:B003/m01); harmful evil eye (quranic:root_001069:B004/m01); amulet or sorcery that restrains sight, marriage, or opinion (quranic:root_000018:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:6, 76:18 (ع ي ن); 76:12 (ج ن ن); 76:13, 76:19-20 (ر ء ي); 76:15, 76:19 (ط و ف); 76:29 (ء خ ذ)
- Synthesis: The motifs form a rival apparatus for reading and controlling hidden causes: vision supplies the sign, jinn or apparition supplies an unseen intermediary, the eye supplies a diagnosis of harm, and the amulet attempts restraint. This usefully pressures the surah's primary claims about revelation, knowledge, and will by staging an alternative route to unseen information and control that the Quranic descent displaces.

### S14. Whole-surah - A Vowed Offering Brought to the Sacred Precinct
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A self-assumed vow is embodied in an animal or property offering, conducted to the sanctuary, and completed through prayer, slaughter, and cooking.
- Active motifs: self-imposed vow or obligation (quranic:root_001488:B002/m01); sacrificial offering brought to Mecca or the sanctuary (quranic:root_001583:B005/m01); sacred territory or a valley at Arafat (quranic:root_000666:B008/m01); prayer place (quranic:root_000675:B002/m01); knife that stills a slaughtered animal (quranic:root_000726:B007/m01); butcher-cook responsible for slaughter and cooking (quranic:root_001205:B007/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:3 (ه د ي); 76:7 (ن ذ ر); 76:8 (س ك ن); 76:16 (ق د ر); 76:26 (س ب ح, س ج د)
- Synthesis: Vow, offering, sacred destination, worship, knife, and butcher-cook form a complete sacrificial rite. This materializes the surah's vow and feeding ethics by making an inward obligation travel as valuable substance and become prepared food; the guidance-root's offering sense also reframes being guided as being conducted toward consecrated transfer.

### S15. Whole-surah - Slaughter, Skinning, Cutting, and Roasting
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An animal is killed with a knife, skinned, divided into flesh and fat, and cooked in a pot or roasted until its grease drips.
- Active motifs: knife that stills the slaughtered animal (quranic:root_000726:B007/m01); butcher-cook handling slaughter and cooking (quranic:root_001205:B007/m01); cooking pot and prepared broth (quranic:root_001205:B007/m02); cutting and splitting flesh (quranic:root_000787:B004/m01); flesh or fat left on a hide during skinning (quranic:root_001102:B012/m01); fatty flesh and abundant marrow (quranic:root_001642:B004/m01); roast meat dripping fat (quranic:root_000787:B005/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:4 (غ ل ل); 76:7, 76:11 (ش ر ر); 76:8 (س ك ن); 76:16 (ق د ر); 76:27 (و ر ي)
- Synthesis: The sequence has distinct craft operations and outcomes: killing arrests movement, skinning separates hide from meat, cutting portions the carcass, and pot or fire converts fatty flesh into broth or roast. This materializes the food given in 76:8-9 by exposing the animal body, skilled labor, and heat behind prepared provision, while the knife's lexical "stilling" usefully pressures the surah's scenes of repose with the violence that food preparation can conceal.

### S16. Whole-surah - Interweaving, Sewing, Dyeing, and Layering Cloth
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Interwoven yarn is joined at its edges, saturated with dye, shaped by a settled tailor, and finished as layered garment or pile fabric.
- Active motifs: interwoven yarns (quranic:root_001426:B004/m01); sewing two hides or cloth sections at a thick edge (quranic:root_000121:B006/m01); dye absorbed into cloth (quranic:root_000722:B009/m01); resident tailor or craftsperson (quranic:root_001215:B014/m01); undergarment or layer between clothes (quranic:root_001102:B007/m01); garment and clothing (quranic:root_000209:B003/m01); pile fabric, cover, or carpet (quranic:root_001241:B007/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2 (ب ص ر, م ش ج); 76:4 (غ ل ل); 76:14 (ق ط ف); 76:15-16 (ق ر ر); 76:17, 76:21 (س ق ي); 76:21 (ث و ب)
- Synthesis: The motifs follow a textile-production sequence from thread structure through joining, coloring, tailoring, layering, and a finished cloth surface. This materializes the surah's green silk and reclining furnishings as made objects rather than static luxury, reinforcing the primary return of accepted work as wearable and habitable craft.

### S17. Whole-surah - Menstrual Signs, Waiting, Washing, and Recovery
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Bodily signs distinguish menstruation from purity, a cycle and waiting interval are counted, washing marks transition, and postpartum recovery closes the process.
- Active motifs: yellow or white trace used to distinguish menstruation from purity (quranic:root_000531:B007/m01); menstrual, pure, or intermenstrual interval (quranic:root_001210:B003/m01); cessation of menstrual blood followed by washing (quranic:root_000953:B002/m01); practical purification with water (quranic:root_000953:B003/m01); holding an enslaved woman until menstruation establishes womb status (quranic:root_001210:B010/m01); counted waiting period (quranic:root_000989:B003/m01); recovery from postpartum bleeding or illness (quranic:root_001042:B011/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:13, 76:19-20 (ر ء ي); 76:21 (ط ه ر, ع ل و); 76:23 (ق ر ء); 76:31 (ع د د)
- Synthesis: Observation, interval, cessation, washing, legally imposed waiting, and recovery form an embodied temporal process. This usefully pressures a frictionless reading of the surah's purity: within the created body, purity can be a discerned transition involving blood and time, while the inspection of an enslaved woman's cycle also exposes how claims of purity and certainty can become instruments of control.

### S18. Whole-surah - A Wheel-and-Pulley Machine Lifts Well Water
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A handle turns an iron axle and pulley, a water wheel or well timber transfers the load, and a beam and bucket handle balance the lifted water.
- Active motifs: rotating handle and iron pulley axle (quranic:root_000610:B006/m01); rotating pulley used to draw water (quranic:root_000143:B006/m01); wheel or timber for irrigation and lifting loads (quranic:root_000987:B003/m01); wooden well beam named by resemblance to an ostrich (quranic:root_001525:B007/m01); bucket handle or side that balances its load (quranic:root_000741:B008/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:2 (س م ع); 76:9 (ر و د); 76:20 (ن ع م); 76:25 (ب ك ر); 76:27 (ع ج ل)
- Synthesis: Handle, axle, pulley, wheel, beam, and bucket form one force-transfer mechanism: rotation redirects effort and balances a rising water load. This materializes the surah's opened springs through the human-scale machinery normally required to raise water, and thereby sharpens the contrast between laborious extraction and the paradisal source made to flow at will.

### S19. P1 -> P2 - The Unmentioned Thing Becomes a Willing Route-Taker
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (76:1-3; canonical scenes 1A-B and 3A) -> P2 (76:28-30; canonical scenes 21A-B and 22A-B); repeated causal sequence and role progression
- Scene or process: The opening moves from unreported thinghood through creation to a shown road; the closing repeats creation and then joins reminder, willing, taking, and the same road.
- Active motifs: thing that can be known and reported (quranic:root_000831:B001/m01); creating and bringing into being (quranic:root_000434:B002/m01); traversable road (quranic:root_000672:B001/m01); reminder restoring presence (quranic:root_000516:B009/m01); will directed to bringing about or attaining a thing (quranic:root_000831:B002/m01); grasping and taking possession (quranic:root_000018:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:1 (ش ي ء, ذ ك ر); 76:2 (خ ل ق); 76:3 (س ب ل); 76:25 (ذ ك ر); 76:28 (ش ي ء, خ ل ق); 76:29 (ش ي ء, س ب ل, ذ ك ر, ء خ ذ); 76:30-31 (ش ي ء)
- Synthesis: The two pericopes repeat creation followed by orientation, but the human role advances. P1 presents an entity brought into reportable existence and shown the road; P2 presents the created entity as the recipient of reminder and the agent who wills and takes that road, while willing remains nested within divine will. The bridge gives the surah an accountable-agency envelope without relocating either creation scene or either path scene from its canonical channel.

### S20. P1 -> P2 - Refused Recompense Returns as Thanked Striving
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (76:8-9; canonical scene 7C) -> P2 (76:21-22; canonical scene 15A); causal sequence and role reversal
- Scene or process: Human providers refuse to turn recipients into sources of repayment or praise; divine evaluation then makes the providers' own striving the object of recompense and thanks.
- Active motifs: corresponding recompense for an act (quranic:root_000244:B001/m01); recognition and praise for a favor (quranic:root_000810:B001/m01); work, earning, and serious effort (quranic:root_000709:B002/m01); reward returning from one's act (quranic:root_000209:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:3 (ش ك ر); 76:9 (ج ز ي, ش ك ر); 76:12 (ج ز ي); 76:21 (ث و ب); 76:22 (ج ز ي, ش ك ر, س ع ي)
- Synthesis: P1 refuses “recompense” and “thanks” from vulnerable beneficiaries; P2 answers with the same two relations from a different source, declaring the gift to be their recompense and their striving thanked. The bridge is therefore not a general reward theme but an exact reversal of creditor and evaluator: the human giver declines a claim on the recipient, and divine recompense returns value to the giver without transferring that debt back to the vulnerable.

### S21. P1 -> P2 - The Cup-Mixture-Spring Signature Returns and Culminates
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (76:5-6; canonical scene 5B) -> P2 (76:17-21; canonical scenes 12A, 12C, and 25A); repeated scene signature and staged culmination
- Scene or process: A cup is defined by its mixture and followed by a spring; the sequence recurs in P2 and culminates when the Lord directly gives a purifying drink.
- Active motifs: cup together with its contents (quranic:root_001277:B001/m01); mixing a drink with another substance (quranic:root_001417:B001/m01); flowing spring source (quranic:root_001069:B006/m01); taking liquid into the body (quranic:root_000783:B001/m01); giving another person drink (quranic:root_000722:B001/m01); water or medium that purifies its recipient (quranic:root_000953:B004/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:5 (ك ء س, م ز ج, ش ر ب); 76:6 (ع ي ن, ش ر ب); 76:17 (ك ء س, م ز ج, س ق ي); 76:18 (ع ي ن); 76:21 (ش ر ب, س ق ي, ط ه ر)
- Synthesis: P1 and P2 share the ordered signature cup -> mixture -> spring, not merely the topic of drinking. The first sequence establishes righteous drinkers and an opened source; the second repeats the syntax inside the reward scene; the final drink changes the operation from access to direct divine service and purification. The bridge records that progression while the hydraulic, serving, and cleansing scenes remain in their canonical local placements.

### S22. P1 -> P2 - Love Turns toward Gift or toward the Immediate
- Reading type: surface-primary
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (76:7-10; canonical scenes 4B and 7B) -> P2 (76:27; canonical scenes 20A-C); causal contrast through a repeated affective role
- Scene or process: Love accompanies relinquishing food while fear keeps the future day active in P1; in P2 love fastens onto the immediate and the future day is left behind.
- Active motifs: enduring attachment or preference (quranic:root_000286:B002/m01); feeding another person (quranic:root_000934:B002/m01); fear that anticipates harm (quranic:root_000447:B001/m01); calamitous event embodied as a day (quranic:root_001700:B003/m01); haste and bringing forward (quranic:root_000987:B001/m01); leaving or abandoning (quranic:root_001638:B003/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:7 (خ و ف, ي و م); 76:8 (ح ب ب, ط ع م); 76:9 (ط ع م); 76:10 (خ و ف, ي و م); 76:11 (ي و م); 76:27 (ح ب ب, ي و م, ع ج ل, و ذ ر)
- Synthesis: The bridge holds love constant and changes its direction and temporal effect. In P1 attachment does not prevent food from passing to another, while fear of the coming day governs present generosity; in P2 attachment selects the immediate, and the heavy day is cut off from attention. This is a causal contrast between two orientations of desire, not a loose pairing of charity and judgment.

### S23. P1 -> P2 - Binding Reverses from Captivity into Ornament and Constitution
- Reading type: mixed
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (76:4, 76:8; canonical scenes 5A and 7A) -> P2 (76:21, 76:28; canonical scenes 15C and 21A); shared spatial invariant with functional reversal and role progression
- Scene or process: External binding first confines a punished body and names a captive; P2 changes encirclement into honored ornament and changes the captivity-root into the internal cohesion of a created body.
- Active motifs: iron collar binding limb or neck (quranic:root_001102:B006/m01); binding and captivity (quranic:root_000030:B001/m01); bracelet encircling the hand (quranic:root_000758:B004/m01); wearable ornament (quranic:root_000353:B001/m01); firm joints and articulated bodily constitution (quranic:root_000030:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:4 (غ ل ل); 76:8 (ء س ر); 76:21 (ح ل ي, س و ر); 76:28 (ء س ر)
- Synthesis: Encirclement and fastening persist while their functions reverse. P1's collar and captive externalize coercive control; P2's bracelet turns enclosure into visible honor, and bodily “binding” turns it inward as the integrity of joints. The shared invariant is therefore not metalwork or the body in general but bounded attachment transformed from deprivation of agency into adornment and created capacity.

### S24. P1 -> P2 - Rewarded Patience Becomes a Commanded Discipline
- Reading type: mixed
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (76:12; canonical scenes 9A-B) -> P2 (76:24; canonical scene 18A); causal sequence and role progression
- Scene or process: Patience is first reported as the completed cause of reward and is later issued as an imperative under the Lord's judgment.
- Active motifs: holding the self from panic or complaint (quranic:root_000840:B001/m01); recompense corresponding to action (quranic:root_000244:B001/m01); just adjudication (quranic:root_000348:B002/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:9 (ج ز ي); 76:12 (ص ب ر, ج ز ي); 76:22 (ج ز ي); 76:24 (ص ب ر, ح ك م); 76:30 (ح ك م)
- Synthesis: P1 looks backward from reward to the patience that caused it; P2 turns the same held posture forward into a present command while judgment is still unfolding. The role progression converts an exemplary completed trait into an ongoing discipline, so the rewarded group supplies the enacted form of what the addressee is then required to sustain.

### S25. P1 -> P2 - Prepared Punishment Frames the Surah
- Reading type: mixed
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (76:4; canonical scene 5A) -> P2 (76:31; canonical scene 23B); repeated scene signature and whole-surah framing recurrence
- Scene or process: Punitive equipment is declared ready near the opening, and prepared painful punishment returns in the final ayah after the intervening alternatives of mercy and reward.
- Active motifs: equipment held ready for need (quranic:root_000978:B001/m01); preparing reserve or equipment for a coming event (quranic:root_000989:B002/m01); kindled blaze (quranic:root_000708:B001/m01); severe punishment (quranic:root_000994:B005/m01); felt pain (quranic:root_000046:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:4 (ع ت د, س ع ر); 76:31 (ع د د, ع ذ ب, ء ل م)
- Synthesis: The repeated operation is preparation: consequence exists as ready apparatus before encounter. P1 gives that readiness material form in restraints and blaze; P2 restores the preparation formula at the close and names pain as its outcome. This recurrence frames the surah's central hospitality and mercy with a non-improvised consequence, without duplicating either local punishment scene.

### S26. P1 -> P2 - Ingratitude Progresses from Response to Capturing Command
- Reading type: mixed
- Pericopes and bridge: P1 (76:3; canonical scene 3B) -> P2 (76:24; canonical scene 18B); role progression and contrast in moral agency
- Scene or process: Covering a received favor first appears as one possible response to guidance; it later appears in a would-be commander whose order must not capture another person's obedience.
- Active motifs: concealment and denial of a favor (quranic:root_001307:B004/m01); compliance and following an order (quranic:root_000956:B001/m01); forcing an obedient person into disobedience (quranic:root_001307:B007/m01); sin as delay from good (quranic:root_000013:B001/m01)
- Ayah anchors: 76:3-5, 76:24 (ك ف ر); 76:24 (ط و ع, ء ث م)
- Synthesis: P1 locates ingratitude inside the guided person's own response; P2 externalizes it as a social pressure issuing commands. The same covered-favor field thus progresses from failed reception to attempted control of someone else's action. The prohibition on obedience reverses that capture, preserving the agent's alignment with guidance rather than allowing another person's ingratitude to become governing authority.
